1: [package]
2: name = "louder_rust"
3: version = "0.1.0"
4: edition = "2024"
5: 
6: [lib]
7: name = "louder_rust"
8: path = "src/lib.rs"
9: # The rlib is what `louder_godot` links against; the cdylib is what the C header
10: # in `tests/` is compiled and run against.
11: crate-type = ["cdylib", "rlib"]
12: 
13: [features]
14: # The shared machinery an adapter crate needs in order to declare Godot classes
15: # of its own, and nothing else. The engine registers no classes itself: it is
16: # reached from Godot, or from any other host, through the C boundary below, and
17: # `godot_api!` lives here only so that every adapter spells registration the
18: # same way.
19: #
20: # With the feature off, `godot` is not compiled at all, so the C header, the C++
21: # tests, and any host that does not care about Godot build against the engine
22: # alone. `louder_godot` turns it on because it is the crate that registers
23: # classes.
24: default = []
25: godot-bindings = ["dep:godot"]
26: 
27: [dependencies]
28: bitflags = "2.13.2"
29: godot = { version = "0.5.5", optional = true }
30: serde_json = "1.0.151"
31: strum = "0.28.0"
32: strum_macros = "0.28.0"

1: //! The dialogue engine.
2: //!
3: //! Each module here holds one part of the engine, and each is reached through an
4: //! ordinary Rust API. None of them knows what host, if any, is driving it: a
5: //! host that wants the classes as Godot types declares them in the `louder_godot`
6: //! adapter over this crate, and a host that wants the `extern "C"` entry points
7: //! finds them in the crate root.
8: //!
9: //! The engine's own vocabulary is in [`context::LouderContext`]: one instance is
10: //! one running dialogue, holding its name table, its conversations, its actors,
11: //! its speaker config, its story state, its event queue, and its ask cache. The
12: //! types it hands out are plain Rust values, so a host is free to read them and
13: //! change them, and nothing here is tied to the shape any particular language
14: //! wants them in.
15: 
16: pub mod actor;
17: pub mod callback;
18: pub mod conversation;
19: pub mod context;
20: pub mod event;
21: pub mod expression;

25: pub mod json_loader;
26: pub mod name;
27: pub mod object;
28: pub mod query;

30: pub mod simulator;

1: // The primary engine interface, defined in docs/engine/index.md
2: //
3: // This crate is the engine and nothing else. The `extern "C"` entry points below
4: // are its whole outward face, and the generated C header is built from them. No
5: // host language is spoken here: an adapter, such as the Godot extension in
6: // `louder_godot`, depends on this crate as an ordinary library and declares its
7: // own classes over it. The only thing this crate keeps for an adapter is the
8: // `godot_api!` macro in `godot_api.rs`, behind the `godot-bindings` feature.
9: //
10: // Strings cross this boundary as bytes, and nothing here decides how they are
11: // encoded. A host that hands over bytes in an encoding the engine does not read
12: // gets them back the same way, and a host that wants text decodes it itself
13: // after the call.
14: 
15: mod test_linkage;
16: 
17: // The `godot_api!` macro an adapter declares its classes with. The module itself
18: // is private; the macro is exported at the crate root, and needs the feature.
19: mod godot_api;
20: 
21: pub mod louder;
22: 
23: use louder::actor::*;
24: use louder::callback::*;
25: use louder::context::*;
26: use louder::event::*;
27: use louder::expression::*;
28: use louder::ffi::*;
29: use louder::hub::*;
30: use louder::json;
31: use louder::json_loader::*;
32: use louder::object::*;
33: use louder::query;
34: use louder::rules::DialogueRules;
35: use louder::name::*;
36: use louder::simulator::*;

39: // Constructors for the tagged unions above.
40: //
41: // cbindgen lowers each of these enums to a C++ struct of a tag and an anonymous
42: // union of bodies, which the FFI offers no way to fill in: a caller that wants
43: // to build an EventData has to know the tag order and the name of the body
44: // member, and gets no compiler help if either is wrong. Building the value here
45: // and returning it keeps that layout inside the engine, so any host can
46: // construct one of these without repeating it.
47: //
48: // A union is only produced here, never consumed: nothing in the engine takes one
49: // as an argument, so there is no constructor for a LouderOption::None. Rust
50: // generics cannot cross the FFI, so LouderOption has one constructor per type
51: // the engine hands back. These are calls rather than constants, so a union
52: // cannot be built in a constexpr or a static initialiser.
53: 
54: /// Build a [`LouderVariant::Int`].
55: #[unsafe(no_mangle)]
56: pub extern "C" fn make_variant_int(value: i32) -> LouderVariant {
57: LouderVariant::from_i32(value)
58: }
59: 
60: /// Build a [`LouderVariant::Float`].
61: #[unsafe(no_mangle)]
62: pub extern "C" fn make_variant_float(value: f32) -> LouderVariant {
63: LouderVariant::from_f32(value)
64: }
65: 
66: /// Build a [`LouderVariant::String`], naming the string rather than carrying it.
67: #[unsafe(no_mangle)]
68: pub extern "C" fn make_variant_string(value: LouderName) -> LouderVariant {
69: LouderVariant::from_name(value)
70: }
71: 
72: /// Build a boolean as a [`LouderVariant::Int`] of 1 or 0.
73: #[unsafe(no_mangle)]
74: pub extern "C" fn make_variant_bool(value: bool) -> LouderVariant {
75: LouderVariant::from_bool(value)
76: }
77: 
78: /// Build the [`LouderVariant::Int`] of 0 that an unwritten variable reads as.
79: #[unsafe(no_mangle)]
80: pub extern "C" fn make_variant_zero() -> LouderVariant {
81: LouderVariant::zero()
82: }
83: 
84: /// Build a [`LouderOption::Some`] holding an event.
85: #[unsafe(no_mangle)]
86: pub extern "C" fn make_some_event(value: LouderEvent) -> LouderOption<LouderEvent> {
87: LouderOption::Some(value)
88: }
89: 
90: /// Build a [`LouderOption::Some`] holding an ask.
91: #[unsafe(no_mangle)]
92: pub extern "C" fn make_some_ask(value: LouderAsk) -> LouderOption<LouderAsk> {
93: LouderOption::Some(value)
94: }
95: 
96: /// Build the [`EventData::None`] variant, which carries nothing.

98: pub extern "C" fn make_event_data_none() -> EventData {
99: EventData::None
100: }

106: }

205: ///
206: /// The engine emits these itself when it cannot read a record of a loaded
207: /// document, so this exists to round out the set rather than because a host
208: /// has a reason to raise one.
209: #[unsafe(no_mangle)]
210: pub extern "C" fn make_event_data_load_error(failure: LoadFailure) -> EventData {

214: #[unsafe(no_mangle)]
215: extern "C" fn test_linkage() -> i32 {
216: test_linkage::test_linkage::test_linkage()
217: }
218: 
219: /// Get the id of a string.
220: ///
221: /// Returns [`LOUDER_NAME_NONE`] if the string was never declared, or if either
222: /// pointer was null.
223: ///
224: /// - `buffer`: the string bytes to look up.
225: /// - `max_size`: the number of bytes to read from `buffer`.
226: #[unsafe(no_mangle)]
227: pub extern "C" fn get_named_id(ctx: *const LouderContext, buffer: *const u8, max_size: usize) -> LouderName {
228: if ctx.is_null() {
229:   return LOUDER_NAME_NONE;
230: }
231: 
232: let Some(content) = read_name_content(buffer, max_size) else {
233:   return LOUDER_NAME_NONE;
234: };
235: 
236: unsafe {
237:   match (*ctx).find_name(&content) {
238:     LouderContextResult::Found(name) => name,
239:     LouderContextResult::NotFound(_) => LOUDER_NAME_NONE,
240:   }
241: }
242: }
243: 
244: /// Assign the id of a string.
245: ///
246: /// If the string was already declared, returns [`LOUDER_NAME_NONE`] and leaves
247: /// the existing id alone. Otherwise a new id is assigned and returned.
248: #[unsafe(no_mangle)]
249: pub extern "C" fn declare_name(ctx: *mut LouderContext, buffer: *const u8, max_size: usize) -> LouderName {
250: if ctx.is_null() {
251:   return LOUDER_NAME_NONE;
252: }
253: 
254: let Some(content) = read_name_content(buffer, max_size) else {
255:   return LOUDER_NAME_NONE;
256: };
257: 
258: unsafe {
259:   match (*ctx).add_name(&content) {
260:     LouderContextResult::Found(_) => LOUDER_NAME_NONE,
261:     LouderContextResult::NotFound(name) => name,
262:   }
263: }
264: }
265: 
266: /// Read the bytes associated with an id into `buffer`.
267: ///
268: /// The bytes come back exactly as they went in, so a name declared in some
269: /// encoding is read in that same one. Decoding them is the host's job, since the
270: /// engine never displays anything itself.
271: ///
272: /// Writes 0 bytes and a terminator if no name is associated with the id, or if
273: /// either pointer was null. Returns the number of bytes written, excluding the
274: /// terminator.
275: #[unsafe(no_mangle)]
276: pub extern "C" fn read_name(ctx: *const LouderContext, name: LouderName, buffer: *mut u8, max_size: usize) -> usize {
277: if ctx.is_null() || buffer.is_null() {
278:   return 0;
279: }
280: 
281: let name_context = unsafe { (*ctx).get_name_content(&name) };
282: let Some(name_context) = name_context else {
283:   return write_bytes(buffer, max_size, &[]);
284: };
285: 
286: write_bytes(buffer, max_size, &name_context.bytes)
287: }
288: 
289: /// Get the event at `index`.
290: ///
291: /// Returns [`LouderOption::None`] once `index` is past the end of the queue,
292: /// or if `ctx` is null. Poll from index 0 upwards until that happens, then
293: /// call [`flush_events`].
294: #[unsafe(no_mangle)]
295: pub extern "C" fn get_event(ctx: *const LouderContext, index: usize) -> LouderOption<LouderEvent> {
296: if ctx.is_null() {
297:   return LouderOption::None
298: }
299: 
300: match unsafe { (*ctx).event(index) } {
301:   Some(event) => LouderOption::Some(event.clone()),
302:   None => LouderOption::None,
303: }
304: }
305: 
306: /// Remove every queued event.
307: ///
308: /// The queue is indexed from 0 again afterwards, so the next [`get_event`] with
309: /// index 0 reads the first event queued after this call.
310: #[unsafe(no_mangle)]
311: pub extern "C" fn flush_events(ctx: *mut LouderContext) {
312: if ctx.is_null() {
313:   return;
314: }
315: 
316: unsafe { (*ctx).clear_events() };
317: }
318: 
319: /// Push an event onto the event queue, used for testing.
320: ///
321: /// The caller fills in the event themselves, so nothing about `code` is
322: /// interpreted here. Returns [`LouderError::Ok`] on success, or
323: /// [`LouderError::Missing`] if either pointer was null.
324: #[unsafe(no_mangle)]
325: pub extern "C" fn test_push_event(
326: ctx: *mut LouderContext,
327: event: *const LouderEvent,
328: ) -> LouderError {
329: if ctx.is_null() || event.is_null() {
330:   return LouderError::Missing;
331: }
332: 
333: unsafe {
334:   (*ctx).push_event((*event).clone());
335: }
336: 
337: LouderError::Ok
338: }
339: 
340: /// Get the ask at `index`.
341: ///
342: /// Asks are not in a stable order, so read them until one comes back
343: /// [`LouderOption::None`] rather than assuming the queue only grows.
344: #[unsafe(no_mangle)]
345: pub extern "C" fn get_ask(ctx: *const LouderContext, index: usize) -> LouderOption<LouderAsk> {
346: if ctx.is_null() {
347:   return LouderOption::None
348: }
349: 
350: let a = unsafe { (*ctx).ask(index).cloned() };
351: 
352: match a {
353:   Some(a) => LouderOption::Some(a),
354:   None => LouderOption::None
355: }
356: }
357: 
358: /// Push an ask onto the ask cache, used for testing.
359: ///
360: /// Returns [`LouderError::Ok`] on success, or [`LouderError::Missing`] if
361: /// either pointer was null.
362: #[unsafe(no_mangle)]
363: pub extern "C" fn push_ask(
364: ctx: *mut LouderContext,
365: ask: *const LouderAsk
366: ) -> LouderError {
367: if ctx.is_null() || ask.is_null() {
368:   return LouderError::Missing;
369: }
370: 
371: unsafe {
372:   (*ctx).push_ask((*ask).clone());
373: }
374: 
375: LouderError::Ok
376: }
377: 
378: /// Answer the ask at `index` with `verdict`.
379: ///
380: /// Answering marks the ask complete and moves it to the back of the cache, so
381: /// indexes shift: read [`get_ask`] again after each answer. Answered asks stay
382: /// readable but no longer accept answers, so a host can simply keep answering
383: /// until [`LouderError::Missing`] comes back and the cache has flushed itself.
384: ///
385: /// Returns [`LouderError::Ok`] if the answer was recorded, or
386: /// [`LouderError::Missing`] if `index` was out of range or that ask had already
387: /// been answered.
388: #[unsafe(no_mangle)]
389: pub extern "C" fn answer_ask(ctx: *mut LouderContext, index: usize, verdict: i32) -> LouderError {
390: if ctx.is_null() {
391:   return LouderError::Missing;
392: }
393: 
394: unsafe {
395:   if (*ctx).answer_ask(index, verdict) {
396:     LouderError::Ok
397:   } else {
398:     LouderError::Missing
399:   }
400: }
401: }
402: 
403: /// Get the flags of a speaker.
404: ///
405: /// A speaker that has not been declared reads as [`SpeakerFlags::empty`], which
406: /// is public by default, so a hub naming it still appears to everyone.
407: #[unsafe(no_mangle)]
408: pub extern "C" fn get_speaker_flags(ctx: *const LouderContext, speaker: LouderName) -> SpeakerFlags {
409: if ctx.is_null() {
410:   return SpeakerFlags::empty();
411: }
412: 
413: unsafe {
414:   match (*ctx).get_speaker(speaker) {
415:     Some(s) => s.flags(),
416:     None => SpeakerFlags::empty(),
417:   }
418: }
419: }
420: 
421: /// Iterate the speakers that `speaker` is listening to.
422: ///
423: /// Pass [`LOUDER_NAME_NONE`] as `start` to begin, then pass the previous result
424: /// until [`LOUDER_NAME_NONE`] comes back. Returns [`LOUDER_NAME_NONE`] for an
425: /// unknown speaker.
426: #[unsafe(no_mangle)]
427: pub extern "C" fn get_next_speaker_listen_to(ctx: *const LouderContext, speaker: LouderName, start: LouderName) -> LouderName {
428: if ctx.is_null() {
429:   return LOUDER_NAME_NONE;
430: }
431: 
432: unsafe { query::next_speaker_listen_to(&*ctx, speaker, start) }
433: }
434: 
435: /// Get a speaker's additional data under `key`.
436: ///
437: /// The speaker config is a singleton rather than a per-conversation object, so
438: /// this takes no conversation. Returns a zero variant if the speaker is
439: /// unknown or has nothing stored under `key`.
440: #[unsafe(no_mangle)]
441: pub extern "C" fn get_speaker_additional(ctx: *const LouderContext, speaker: LouderName, key: LouderName) -> LouderVariant {
442: if ctx.is_null() {
443:   return LouderVariant::Int(0);
444: }
445: 
446: unsafe {
447:   match (*ctx).get_speaker(speaker).and_then(|s| s.additional_get(key)) {
448:     Some(value) => value.clone(),
449:     None => LouderVariant::Int(0),
450:   }
451: }
452: }
453: 
454: /// Get the channel of an actor.

456: /// Actors with [`ActorFlags::SPECTATOR`] or [`ActorFlags::DEBUG`] are not part
457: /// of the flow and read as [`LOUDER_NAME_NONE`].

459: pub extern "C" fn get_actor_channel(ctx: *const LouderContext, conversation: LouderName, actor: LouderActor) -> LouderName {
460: if ctx.is_null() {
461:   return LOUDER_NAME_NONE;
462: }
463: 
464: unsafe {
465:   match (*ctx).get_actor(conversation, actor) {
466:     Some(a) => a.channel(),
467:     None => LOUDER_NAME_NONE,
468:   }
469: }
470: }
471:

477: if ctx.is_null() {
478:   return BLANK_POSITION;
479: }
480: 
481: unsafe {

484:     None => BLANK_POSITION,
485:   }
486: }
487: }

494: if ctx.is_null() {
495:   return ActorFlags::empty();

503: }
504: }

506: /// Get the priority of an actor.

511: if ctx.is_null() {

513: }

523: /// Get an actor's additional data under `key`.
524: ///
525: /// Returns a zero variant if the actor does not exist or has nothing stored

527: #[unsafe(no_mangle)]
528: pub extern "C" fn get_actor_additional(ctx: *const LouderContext, conversation: LouderName, actor: LouderActor, key: LouderName) -> LouderVariant {
529: if ctx.is_null() {
530:   return LouderVariant::Int(0);
531: }
532: 
533: unsafe {

535:     Some(value) => value.clone(),
536:     None => LouderVariant::Int(0),
537:   }
538: }
539: }

541: /// Iterate the speakers an actor speaks as.
542: ///
543: /// Pass [`LOUDER_NAME_NONE`] as `start` to begin, then pass the previous result
544: /// until [`LOUDER_NAME_NONE`] comes back.
545: #[unsafe(no_mangle)]
546: pub extern "C" fn next_actor_speak_as(ctx: *const LouderContext, conversation: LouderName, actor: LouderActor, start: LouderName) -> LouderName {
547: if ctx.is_null() {
548:   return LOUDER_NAME_NONE;

552: }

554: /// Iterate the base speakers an actor listens to.
555: ///
556: /// These are the actor's own `listen_to` speakers, without the ones reachable
557: /// through who it speaks as. See [`next_actor_listening`] for the wider set.
558: #[unsafe(no_mangle)]
559: pub extern "C" fn next_actor_listening_base(ctx: *const LouderContext, conversation: LouderName, actor: LouderActor, start: LouderName) -> LouderName {
560: if ctx.is_null() {
561:   return LOUDER_NAME_NONE;
562: }
563: 
564: unsafe { query::next_actor_listening_base(&*ctx, conversation, actor, start) }
565: }
566: 
567: /// Iterate the speakers an actor listens to.
568: ///
569: /// Includes the actor's `listen_to` speakers plus the listening speakers of the
570: /// speakers it speaks as. Pass [`LOUDER_NAME_NONE`] as `start` to begin.
571: #[unsafe(no_mangle)]
572: pub extern "C" fn next_actor_listening(ctx: *const LouderContext, conversation: LouderName, actor: LouderActor, start: LouderName) -> LouderName {
573: if ctx.is_null() {
574:   return LOUDER_NAME_NONE;
575: }
576: 
577: unsafe { query::next_actor_listening(&*ctx, conversation, actor, start) }
578: }
579: 
580: /// Iterate the actors in a channel.
581: ///
582: /// Pass [`LOUDER_ACTOR_NONE`] as `start` to begin. Actors outside the flow have
583: /// no channel and are never returned.
584: #[unsafe(no_mangle)]
585: pub extern "C" fn next_actor_in_channel(ctx: *const LouderContext, conversation: LouderName, channel: LouderName, start: LouderActor) -> LouderActor {
586: if ctx.is_null() {
587:   return LOUDER_ACTOR_NONE;

590: unsafe { query::next_actor_in_channel(&*ctx, conversation, channel, start) }
591: }
592: 
593: /// Iterate the actors in a channel at a specific position.
594: ///
595: /// Pass [`LOUDER_ACTOR_NONE`] as `start` to begin.
596: #[unsafe(no_mangle)]
597: pub extern "C" fn next_actor_in_channel_and_position(ctx: *const LouderContext, conversation: LouderName, channel: LouderName, position: LouderPosition, start: LouderActor) -> LouderActor {
598: if ctx.is_null() {
599:   return LOUDER_ACTOR_NONE;

602: unsafe { query::next_actor_in_channel_and_position(&*ctx, conversation, channel, position, start) }
603: }

605: /// Iterate the actors speaking as a speaker.
606: ///
607: /// Pass [`LOUDER_ACTOR_NONE`] as `start` to begin.
608: #[unsafe(no_mangle)]
609: pub extern "C" fn next_actor_speaking_as(ctx: *const LouderContext, conversation: LouderName, speaker: LouderName, start: LouderActor) -> LouderActor {
610: if ctx.is_null() {
611:   return LOUDER_ACTOR_NONE;

615: }

617: /// Iterate the actors listening to a speaker.
618: ///
619: /// This covers `listen_to` plus the listening actors derived from the listening
620: /// speakers of the speakers an actor speaks as. Pass [`LOUDER_ACTOR_NONE`] as

622: #[unsafe(no_mangle)]
623: pub extern "C" fn next_actor_listening_to(ctx: *const LouderContext, conversation: LouderName, speaker: LouderName, start: LouderActor) -> LouderActor {
624: if ctx.is_null() {

629: }

631: /// Iterate the actors matching certain flags.
632: ///
633: /// With `exact` nonzero the flags must match exactly; otherwise every bit in
634: /// `include` must be present. Pass [`LOUDER_ACTOR_NONE`] as `start` to begin.
635: #[unsafe(no_mangle)]
636: pub extern "C" fn next_actor_from_flags(ctx: *const LouderContext, conversation: LouderName, include: ActorFlags, exact: i32, start: LouderActor) -> LouderActor {
637: if ctx.is_null() {
638:   return LOUDER_ACTOR_NONE;
639: }
640: 
641: unsafe { query::next_actor_from_flags(&*ctx, conversation, include, exact, start) }
642: }

644: /// Iterate the tags of a position.
645: ///
646: /// Pass [`LOUDER_NAME_NONE`] as `start` to begin.
647: #[unsafe(no_mangle)]
648: pub extern "C" fn next_hub_tag(ctx: *const LouderContext, conversation: LouderName, position: LouderPosition, start: LouderName) -> LouderName {
649: if ctx.is_null() {
650:   return LOUDER_NAME_NONE;
651: }
652: 
653: unsafe { query::next_hub_tag(&*ctx, conversation, position, start) }
654: }

656: /// Get the speaker of a hub.
657: ///
658: /// Returns [`LOUDER_NAME_NONE`] if the hub has no speaker, which means it is
659: /// visible to everyone, or if the hub does not exist.
660: #[unsafe(no_mangle)]
661: pub extern "C" fn get_hub_speaker(ctx: *const LouderContext, conversation: LouderName, position: LouderPosition) -> LouderName {
662: if ctx.is_null() {
663:   return LOUDER_NAME_NONE;
664: }
665: 
666: unsafe {
667:   match (*ctx).hub(conversation, position) {
668:     Some(hub) => hub.speaker(),
669:     None => LOUDER_NAME_NONE,
670:   }
671: }
672: }
673: 
674: /// Get the speaker of a hub, as an actor would hear it.
675: ///
676: /// A hub with a speaker of its own speaks as that speaker. A hub without one is
677: /// anonymous: it speaks as the first voice in the speak as list of whoever is
678: /// standing on it, so the answer depends on the actor, and a hub that has no
679: /// speaker of its own answers differently for each of them.
680: ///
681: /// Returns [`LOUDER_NAME_NONE`] if the hub does not exist, or if the actor has
682: /// no voice to speak it with.
683: #[unsafe(no_mangle)]
684: pub extern "C" fn get_hub_speaker_for_actor(
685:   ctx: *const LouderContext,
686:   conversation: LouderName,
687:   position: LouderPosition,
688:   actor_perspective: LouderActor,
689: ) -> LouderName {
690: if ctx.is_null() {
691:   return LOUDER_NAME_NONE;
692: }
693: 
694: unsafe { Simulator::hub_speaker_for(&*ctx, conversation, actor_perspective, position) }
695: }
696: 
697: /// Get the text of a hub as a name.
698: ///
699: /// Returns [`LOUDER_NAME_NONE`] if the hub has no text or does not exist. Read
700: /// the string back with [`read_name`].
701: #[unsafe(no_mangle)]
702: pub extern "C" fn get_hub_text(ctx: *const LouderContext, conversation: LouderName, position: LouderPosition) -> LouderName {
703: if ctx.is_null() {
704:   return LOUDER_NAME_NONE;
705: }
706: 
707: unsafe {
708:   match (*ctx).hub(conversation, position) {
709:     Some(hub) => hub.text_name(),
710:     None => LOUDER_NAME_NONE,
711:   }
712: }
713: }
714: 
715: /// Get the number of choices on a hub from an actor's perspective.
716: ///
717: /// Only choices the actor could actually take are counted, so this applies the
718: /// visibility, recipient, and spoke rules. Returns 0 for an unknown hub or
719: /// actor.
720: #[unsafe(no_mangle)]
721: pub extern "C" fn get_hub_choice_count(ctx: *const LouderContext, conversation: LouderName, position: LouderPosition, actor_perspective: LouderActor) -> i32 {
722: if ctx.is_null() {
723:   return 0;
724: }
725: 
726: unsafe {
727:   Simulator::available_choices(&*ctx, conversation, actor_perspective, position).len() as i32
728: }
729: }
730: 
731: /// Get the text of a choice on a hub as a name.
732: ///
733: /// A choice has no text of its own, so this is the text of the hub it links to.
734: /// Returns [`LOUDER_NAME_NONE`] if the choice does not exist or is not
735: /// available to `actor_perspective`.
736: #[unsafe(no_mangle)]
737: pub extern "C" fn get_hub_choice_text(ctx: *const LouderContext, conversation: LouderName, position: LouderPosition, actor_perspective: LouderActor, choice_index: i32) -> LouderName {
738: if ctx.is_null() || choice_index < 0 {
739:   return LOUDER_NAME_NONE;
740: }
741: 
742: unsafe {
743:   match query::available_choice(&*ctx, conversation, position, actor_perspective, choice_index as usize) {
744:     Some(choice) => choice.text_name(),
745:     None => LOUDER_NAME_NONE,
746:   }
747: }
748: }
749: 
750: /// Get the position a choice on a hub links to.
751: ///
752: /// Returns [`BLANK_POSITION`] if the choice does not exist or is not available
753: /// to `actor_perspective`.
754: #[unsafe(no_mangle)]
755: pub extern "C" fn get_hub_choice_link(ctx: *const LouderContext, conversation: LouderName, position: LouderPosition, actor_perspective: LouderActor, choice_index: i32) -> i32 {
756: if ctx.is_null() || choice_index < 0 {
757:   return BLANK_POSITION;
758: }
759: 
760: unsafe {
761:   match query::available_choice(&*ctx, conversation, position, actor_perspective, choice_index as usize) {
762:     Some(choice) => choice.position(),
763:     None => BLANK_POSITION,
764:   }
765: }
766: }
767: 
768: /// Get the expressions associated with a hub as a name.
769: ///
770: /// The schema stores expressions newline separated; this returns the whole
771: /// block as one name, so the caller splits it to get individual expressions.
772: /// Returns [`LOUDER_NAME_NONE`] if the hub has none.
773: #[unsafe(no_mangle)]
774: pub extern "C" fn get_hub_expressions(ctx: *const LouderContext, conversation: LouderName, position: LouderPosition) -> LouderName {
775: if ctx.is_null() {
776:   return LOUDER_NAME_NONE;
777: }
778: 
779: unsafe {
780:   match (*ctx).hub(conversation, position) {
781:     Some(hub) => hub.expressions_joined(),
782:     None => LOUDER_NAME_NONE,
783:   }
784: }
785: }
786: 
787: /// Get the callbacks associated with a hub as a name.
788: ///
789: /// The schema stores callbacks newline separated; this returns the whole block
790: /// as one name. Returns [`LOUDER_NAME_NONE`] if the hub has none.
791: #[unsafe(no_mangle)]
792: pub extern "C" fn get_hub_callbacks(ctx: *const LouderContext, conversation: LouderName, position: LouderPosition) -> LouderName {
793: if ctx.is_null() {
794:   return LOUDER_NAME_NONE;
795: }
796: 
797: unsafe {
798:   match (*ctx).hub(conversation, position) {
799:     Some(hub) => hub.callbacks_joined(),
800:     None => LOUDER_NAME_NONE,
801:   }
802: }
803: }
804: 
805: /// Get the flags of a hub.
806: ///
807: /// Returns [`HubFlags::empty`] if the hub does not exist.
808: #[unsafe(no_mangle)]
809: pub extern "C" fn get_hub_flags(ctx: *const LouderContext, conversation: LouderName, position: LouderPosition) -> HubFlags {
810: if ctx.is_null() {
811:   return HubFlags::empty();
812: }
813: 
814: unsafe {
815:   match (*ctx).hub(conversation, position) {
816:     Some(hub) => hub.flags(),
817:     None => HubFlags::empty(),
818:   }
819: }
820: }
821: 
822: /// Iterate the recipients of a hub.
823: ///
824: /// Returns [`LOUDER_NAME_NONE`] when the hub has no recipients, which means the
825: /// hub is not restricted. Pass [`LOUDER_NAME_NONE`] as `start` to begin.
826: #[unsafe(no_mangle)]
827: pub extern "C" fn next_hub_recipient(ctx: *const LouderContext, conversation: LouderName, position: LouderPosition, start: LouderName) -> LouderName {
828: if ctx.is_null() {
829:   return LOUDER_NAME_NONE;
830: }
831: 
832: unsafe { query::next_hub_recipient(&*ctx, conversation, position, start) }
833: }
834: 
835: /// Get the channel of a hub.
836: ///
837: /// Returns [`LOUDER_NAME_NONE`] if the hub does not change the channel, in
838: /// which case an actor keeps whatever channel it already had.
839: #[unsafe(no_mangle)]
840: pub extern "C" fn get_hub_channel(ctx: *const LouderContext, conversation: LouderName, position: LouderPosition) -> LouderName {
841: if ctx.is_null() {
842:   return LOUDER_NAME_NONE;
843: }
844: 
845: unsafe {
846:   match (*ctx).hub(conversation, position) {
847:     Some(hub) => hub.channel(),
848:     None => LOUDER_NAME_NONE,
849:   }
850: }
851: }
852: 
853: /// Get the priority of a hub.
854: ///
855: /// Returns 0 if the hub does not exist, which is also the default priority.
856: #[unsafe(no_mangle)]
857: pub extern "C" fn get_hub_priority(ctx: *const LouderContext, conversation: LouderName, position: LouderPosition) -> i32 {
858: if ctx.is_null() {
859:   return 0;
860: }
861: 
862: unsafe {
863:   match (*ctx).hub(conversation, position) {
864:     Some(hub) => hub.priority(),
865:     None => 0,
866:   }
867: }
868: }
869: 
870: /// Get a hub's additional data under `key`.
871: ///
872: /// Returns a zero variant if the hub does not exist or has nothing stored
873: /// under `key`.
874: #[unsafe(no_mangle)]
875: pub extern "C" fn get_hub_additional(ctx: *const LouderContext, conversation: LouderName, position: LouderPosition, key: LouderName) -> LouderVariant {
876: if ctx.is_null() {
877:   return LouderVariant::Int(0);
878: }
879: 
880: unsafe {
881:   match (*ctx)
882:     .hub(conversation, position)
883:     .and_then(|hub| hub.additional_get(key))
884:   {
885:     Some(value) => value.clone(),
886:     None => LouderVariant::Int(0),
887:   }
888: }
889: }
890: 
891: /// Iterate the hubs in a channel.
892: ///
893: /// Hubs that name no channel belong to the default channel, so searching for it
894: /// also finds them. Pass [`BLANK_POSITION`] as `start` to begin.
895: #[unsafe(no_mangle)]
896: pub extern "C" fn next_hub_by_channel(ctx: *const LouderContext, conversation: LouderName, channel: LouderName, start: LouderPosition) -> LouderPosition {
897: if ctx.is_null() {
898:   return BLANK_POSITION;
899: }
900: 
901: unsafe { query::next_hub_by_channel(&*ctx, conversation, channel, start) }
902: }
903: 
904: /// Iterate the hubs spoken by a speaker.
905: ///
906: /// Pass [`BLANK_POSITION`] as `start` to begin.
907: #[unsafe(no_mangle)]
908: pub extern "C" fn next_hub_spoken_by(ctx: *const LouderContext, conversation: LouderName, speaker: LouderName, start: LouderPosition) -> LouderPosition {
909: if ctx.is_null() {
910:   return BLANK_POSITION;
911: }
912: 
913: unsafe { query::next_hub_spoken_by(&*ctx, conversation, speaker, start) }
914: }
915: 
916: /// Iterate the hubs heard by a speaker.
917: ///
918: /// This searches the speaker's own listening speakers as well as the speaker
919: /// itself, which are the hubs that may appear to an actor speaking as this
920: /// speaker. Pass [`BLANK_POSITION`] as `start` to begin.
921: #[unsafe(no_mangle)]
922: pub extern "C" fn next_hub_heard_by(ctx: *const LouderContext, conversation: LouderName, speaker: LouderName, start: LouderPosition) -> LouderPosition {
923: if ctx.is_null() {
924:   return BLANK_POSITION;
925: }
926: 
927: unsafe { query::next_hub_heard_by(&*ctx, conversation, speaker, start) }
928: }
929: 
930: /// Iterate the hubs with a priority within `[priority_min, priority_max]`.
931: ///
932: /// The bounds are inclusive. Pass [`BLANK_POSITION`] as `start` to begin.
933: #[unsafe(no_mangle)]
934: pub extern "C" fn next_hub_by_priority(ctx: *const LouderContext, conversation: LouderName, priority_min: i32, priority_max: i32, start: LouderPosition) -> LouderPosition {
935: if ctx.is_null() {
936:   return BLANK_POSITION;
937: }
938: 
939: unsafe { query::next_hub_by_priority(&*ctx, conversation, priority_min, priority_max, start) }
940: }
941: 
942: /// Iterate the hubs containing all, or exactly, the given tags.
943: ///
944: /// With `exact` nonzero the hub must carry exactly the listed tags; otherwise
945: /// carrying extra tags is fine. Pass [`BLANK_POSITION`] as `start` to begin.
946: #[unsafe(no_mangle)]
947: pub extern "C" fn next_hub_from_tags(ctx: *const LouderContext, conversation: LouderName, include: *const LouderName, include_count: i32, exact: i32, start: LouderPosition) -> LouderPosition {
948: if ctx.is_null() {
949:   return BLANK_POSITION;
950: }
951: 
952: let Some(wanted) = read_name_list(include, include_count) else {
953:   return BLANK_POSITION;
954: };
955: 
956: unsafe { query::next_hub_from_tags(&*ctx, conversation, &wanted, exact != 0, start) }
957: }
958: 
959: /// Iterate the hubs matching certain flags.
960: ///
961: /// With `exact` nonzero the flags must match exactly; otherwise every bit in
962: /// `include` must be present. Pass [`BLANK_POSITION`] as `start` to begin.
963: #[unsafe(no_mangle)]
964: pub extern "C" fn next_hub_from_flags(ctx: *const LouderContext, conversation: LouderName, include: HubFlags, exact: i32, start: LouderPosition) -> LouderPosition {
965: if ctx.is_null() {
966:   return BLANK_POSITION;
967: }
968: 
969: unsafe { query::next_hub_from_flags(&*ctx, conversation, include, exact, start) }
970: }
971: 
972: /// Get a story state value under `state`.
973: ///
974: /// The variable's own name decides where it is stored: a leading underscore
975: /// means it is not saved, and an uppercase first letter means it is shared
976: /// across conversations. Variables that have never been written read as 0.
977: /// Reading from a conversation that is not loaded also reads as 0.
978: #[unsafe(no_mangle)]
979: pub extern "C" fn get_story_state(ctx: *const LouderContext, conversation: LouderName, state: LouderName) -> LouderVariant {
980: if ctx.is_null() {
981:   return LouderVariant::Int(0);
982: }
983: 
984: unsafe { (*ctx).get_state(conversation, state) }
985: }
986: 
987: /// Set a story state value.
988: ///
989: /// The variable's own name decides where it is stored, as in
990: /// [`get_story_state`]. Doing nothing if `ctx` is null.
991: #[unsafe(no_mangle)]
992: pub extern "C" fn set_story_state(ctx: *mut LouderContext, conversation: LouderName, key: LouderName, value: LouderVariant) {
993: if ctx.is_null() {
994:   return;
995: }
996: 
997: unsafe {
998:   (*ctx).set_state(conversation, key, value);
999: }
1000: }
1001: 
1002: /// Serialize the story state in JSON into `buffer`.
1003: ///
1004: /// Every bucket is included: the conversation's own saved and unsaved
1005: /// variables, and the shared ones. Keys are written in ascending id order so
1006: /// the output is reproducible.
1007: ///
1008: /// The document is written as UTF-8. Returns the number of bytes written
1009: /// excluding the terminator; a negative value means the conversation is not
1010: /// loaded.
1011: #[unsafe(no_mangle)]
1012: pub extern "C" fn read_state_json(ctx: *const LouderContext, conversation: LouderName, buffer: *mut u8, max_size: usize) -> i32 {
1013: if ctx.is_null() {
1014:   return -1;
1015: }
1016: 
1017: unsafe {
1018:   write_bytes(buffer, max_size, json::state(&*ctx, conversation).as_bytes()) as i32
1019: }
1020: }
1021: 
1022: /// Serialize the story state, along with the positions and details of every
1023: /// actor, in JSON into `buffer`.
1024: ///
1025: /// The document has a `state` object and an `actors` array, both ordered so
1026: /// that the output is reproducible.
1027: ///
1028: /// The document is written as UTF-8. Returns the number of bytes written
1029: /// excluding the terminator; a negative value means the conversation is not
1030: /// loaded.
1031: #[unsafe(no_mangle)]
1032: pub extern "C" fn read_snapshot_json(ctx: *const LouderContext, conversation: LouderName, buffer: *mut u8, max_size: usize) -> i32 {
1033: if ctx.is_null() {
1034:   return -1;
1035: }
1036: 
1037: unsafe {
1038:   match json::snapshot(&*ctx, conversation) {
1039:     Some(document) => write_bytes(buffer, max_size, document.as_bytes()) as i32,
1040:     None => -1,
1041:   }
1042: }
1043: }
1044: 
1045: /// Initialize the engine and return a new context.
1046: ///
1047: /// Initializes the name table, the event queue, and the ask cache. Release the
1048: /// returned handle with [`deinit_louder`]. Returns null only if allocation
1049: /// failed.
1050: #[unsafe(no_mangle)]
1051: pub extern "C" fn init_louder() -> *mut LouderContext {
1052: Box::into_raw(Box::new(LouderContext::new()))
1053: }
1054: 
1055: /// Release a context and everything it holds.
1056: ///
1057: /// Returns [`LouderError::Ok`] on success, or [`LouderError::Missing`] if
1058: /// `ctx` was null. Every conversation, speaker, actor, and queued event goes
1059: /// with it.
1060: #[unsafe(no_mangle)]
1061: pub extern "C" fn deinit_louder(ctx: *mut LouderContext) -> LouderError {
1062: if ctx.is_null() {
1063:   return LouderError::Missing;
1064: }
1065: unsafe {
1066:   drop(Box::from_raw(ctx));
1067: }
1068: LouderError::Ok
1069: }
1070: 
1071: /// Load a conversation from a JSON document in `buffer`.
1072: ///
1073: /// The buffer holds `max_size` bytes. JSON is text, so those bytes have to be
1074: /// UTF-8 for the engine to read them; [`LOUDER_LOAD_MALFORMED_JSON`] is returned
1075: /// if they are not, and re-encoding them is the host's job rather than the
1076: /// engine's.
1077: ///
1078: /// - The JSON is expected in array form; [`LOUDER_LOAD_NOT_A_LIST`] is
1079: ///   returned otherwise.
1080: /// - Standard JSON syntax errors are returned as
1081: ///   [`LOUDER_LOAD_MALFORMED_JSON`].
1082: /// - Standard properties are left default or read from matching JSON fields.
1083: /// - Additional JSON fields are treated as additional data on hubs.
1084: /// - [`LOUDER_LOAD_ALREADY_LOADED`] is returned if `conversation` is already
1085: ///   occupied by another loaded conversation.
1086: /// - Problems with individual records do not fail the load. They are emitted as
1087: ///   events instead, carrying the offending hub's position in
1088: ///   [`Motion::source_position`] and the [`LoadError`] in
1089: ///   [`Motion::target_position`].
1090: #[unsafe(no_mangle)]
1091: pub extern "C" fn load_conversation_json(ctx: *mut LouderContext, conversation: LouderName, buffer: *mut u8, max_size: usize) -> LoadError {
1092: if ctx.is_null() || buffer.is_null() {
1093:   return LOUDER_LOAD_MALFORMED_JSON;
1094: }
1095: 
1096: let bytes = unsafe { std::slice::from_raw_parts(buffer, max_size) };
1097: let document: &str = match decode(bytes) {
1098:   Ok(document) => document,
1099:   Err(error) => return error,
1100: };
1101: 
1102: unsafe { louder::json_loader::load_conversation(&mut *ctx, conversation, document) }
1103: }
1104: 
1105: /// Remove a conversation and all associated actors.
1106: ///
1107: /// Returns 0 on success, or 1 if the conversation was not loaded. Shared story
1108: /// state is kept, since it outlives any one conversation.
1109: #[unsafe(no_mangle)]
1110: pub extern "C" fn remove_conversation(ctx: *mut LouderContext, conversation: LouderName) -> i32 {
1111: if ctx.is_null() {
1112:   return 1;
1113: }
1114: 
1115: unsafe {
1116:   if (*ctx).remove_conversation(conversation) { 0 } else { 1 }
1117: }
1118: }
1119: 
1120: /// Load the conversation config from a JSON document in `buffer`.
1121: ///
1122: /// - The bytes have to be UTF-8 and the error scheme is the one in
1123: ///   [`load_conversation_json`], with speakers as the target objects instead of
1124: ///   hubs.
1125: /// - The config is a singleton as opposed to a named conversation instance,
1126: ///   so this takes no conversation name.
1127: /// - Objects are speakers and their configurations. A speaker already in the
1128: ///   config is replaced rather than treated as an error.
1129: #[unsafe(no_mangle)]
1130: pub extern "C" fn load_conversation_config(ctx: *mut LouderContext, buffer: *mut u8, max_size: usize) -> LoadError {
1131: if ctx.is_null() || buffer.is_null() {
1132:   return LOUDER_LOAD_MALFORMED_JSON;
1133: }
1134: 
1135: let bytes = unsafe { std::slice::from_raw_parts(buffer, max_size) };
1136: let document = match decode(bytes) {
1137:   Ok(document) => document,
1138:   Err(error) => return error,
1139: };
1140: 
1141: unsafe { louder::json_loader::load_config(&mut *ctx, document) }
1142: }
1143: 
1144: /// Remove all speakers in the config.
1145: ///
1146: /// Returns 0 on success, or 1 if `ctx` was null. Conversations and actors are
1147: /// untouched, so hubs naming a now-missing speaker become public again.
1148: #[unsafe(no_mangle)]
1149: pub extern "C" fn clear_config(ctx: *mut LouderContext) -> i32 {
1150: if ctx.is_null() {
1151:   return 1;
1152: }
1153: 
1154: unsafe {
1155:   (*ctx).clear_config();
1156: }
1157: 
1158: 0
1159: }
1160: 
1161: /// Read the dialogue rules currently in effect.
1162: ///
1163: /// A fresh context has every rule on. Returns an empty set if `ctx` was null,
1164: /// since a null context is not running any dialogue.
1165: #[unsafe(no_mangle)]
1166: pub extern "C" fn get_dialogue_rules(ctx: *const LouderContext) -> DialogueRules {
1167: if ctx.is_null() {
1168:   return DialogueRules::empty();
1169: }
1170: 
1171: unsafe { (*ctx).rules() }
1172: }
1173: 
1174: /// Replace the dialogue rules.
1175: ///
1176: /// A rule takes effect on the next move rather than being applied to the
1177: /// conversation as it stands, so a host can turn one off while an actor is
1178: /// standing on a hub it would otherwise resolve through.
1179: ///
1180: /// Bits outside [`DialogueRules`] are ignored rather than refused, so a host
1181: /// carrying a configuration written for a newer engine still gets the rules
1182: /// this build knows about.
1183: #[unsafe(no_mangle)]
1184: pub extern "C" fn set_dialogue_rules(ctx: *mut LouderContext, rules: DialogueRules) -> i32 {
1185: if ctx.is_null() {
1186:   return 1;
1187: }
1188: 
1189: unsafe {
1190:   (*ctx).set_rules(DialogueRules::from_bits_truncate(rules.bits()));
1191: }
1192: 
1193: 0
1194: }
1195: 
1196: /// Add an actor to a conversation and return the integer id'ing it.
1197: ///
1198: /// The actor starts at `start_position` on the default channel, with priority
1199: /// 0. Ids are per conversation and are never reused, so ascending order is a
1200: /// stable iteration order.
1201: ///
1202: /// - `speak_as` and `listen_to` are arrays of `count` names; either may be null
1203: ///   when its count is 0.
1204: /// - [`ActorFlags::SPECTATOR`] and [`ActorFlags::DEBUG`] actors are given no
1205: ///   channel or priority.
1206: ///
1207: /// Emits an [`EventData::ActorAdded`] event on success. Returns the new actor
1208: /// id, or [`LOUDER_ACTOR_NONE`] if the conversation is not loaded or a pointer
1209: /// was null.
1210: #[unsafe(no_mangle)]
1211: pub extern "C" fn add_actor(ctx: *mut LouderContext, conversation: LouderName, start_position: LouderPosition, flags: ActorFlags, speak_as: *const LouderName, speak_as_count: i32, listen_to: *const LouderName, listen_to_count: i32) -> i32 {
1212: if ctx.is_null() {
1213:   return LOUDER_ACTOR_NONE;
1214: }
1215: 
1216: let (Some(speak_as_list), Some(listen_to_list)) = (read_name_list(speak_as, speak_as_count), read_name_list(listen_to, listen_to_count)) else {
1217:   return LOUDER_ACTOR_NONE;
1218: };
1219: 
1220: unsafe {
1221:   if (*ctx).get_conversation(conversation).is_none() {
1222:     return LOUDER_ACTOR_NONE;
1223:   }
1224: 
1225:   let main = (*ctx).main_channel();
1226:   let actor = Actor::new(start_position, flags, speak_as_list, listen_to_list, main);
1227:   (*ctx).add_actor(conversation, actor)
1228: }
1229: }
1230: 
1231: /// Remove an actor from a conversation.
1232: ///
1233: /// Returns 0 on success, or 1 if the actor or conversation did not exist.
1234: /// Emits an [`EventData::ActorRemoved`] event on success.
1235: #[unsafe(no_mangle)]
1236: pub extern "C" fn remove_actor(ctx: *mut LouderContext, conversation: LouderName, actor: LouderActor) -> i32 {
1237: if ctx.is_null() {
1238:   return 1;
1239: }
1240: 
1241: if unsafe { (*ctx).remove_actor(conversation, actor) } {
1242:   0
1243: } else {
1244:   1
1245: }
1246: }
1247: 
1248: /// Select a choice on an actor's current hub.
1249: ///
1250: /// The choice must be one the actor could actually take; see
1251: /// [`get_hub_choice_count`] for what that means. Auto hubs along the way are
1252: /// resolved by taking the first choice available at each one.
1253: ///
1254: /// Returns 0 on success, or 1 if the actor, hub, or choice was missing, if the
1255: /// choice is not available to this actor, or if an auto hub ran out of choices.
1256: #[unsafe(no_mangle)]
1257: pub extern "C" fn select_choice(ctx: *mut LouderContext, conversation: LouderName, actor: LouderActor, choice_index: i32) -> i32 {
1258: if ctx.is_null() {
1259:   return 1;
1260: }
1261: 
1262: unsafe {
1263:   match Simulator::select_choice(&mut *ctx, conversation, actor, choice_index) {
1264:     Ok(_) => 0,
1265:     Err(_) => 1,
1266:   }
1267: }
1268: }
1269: 
1270: /// Move an actor to another position as if it had moved through the network.
1271: ///
1272: /// - `fake_priority` is unused if `0`, otherwise overrides the priority of the
1273: ///   destination. Because 0 is the ordinary default priority, this cannot force
1274: ///   an actor's priority *to* 0 over a hub that sets one.
1275: /// - `fake_channel` is unused if [`LOUDER_NAME_NONE`], otherwise overrides the
1276: ///   channel of the destination.
1277: ///
1278: /// Other actors sharing the actor's channel may be pulled along, exactly as if
1279: /// the actor had chosen. Returns 0 on success, or 1 if the conversation, actor,
1280: /// or destination was missing, or if an auto hub ran out of choices.
1281: #[unsafe(no_mangle)]
1282: pub extern "C" fn force_warp(ctx: *mut LouderContext, conversation: LouderName, actor: LouderActor, target_position: LouderPosition, fake_channel: LouderName, fake_priority: i32) -> i32 {
1283: if ctx.is_null() {
1284:   return 1;
1285: }
1286: 
1287: unsafe {
1288:   match Simulator::force_move(&mut *ctx, conversation, actor, target_position, MotionKind::Warp, fake_channel, fake_priority) {
1289:     Ok(_) => 0,
1290:     Err(_) => 1,
1291:   }
1292: }
1293: }
1294: 
1295: /// Move an actor to another position directly.
1296: ///
1297: /// - `fake_priority` is unused if `0`, otherwise overrides the priority of the
1298: ///   destination. Because 0 is the ordinary default priority, this cannot force
1299: ///   an actor's priority *to* 0 over a hub that sets one.
1300: /// - `fake_channel` is unused if [`LOUDER_NAME_NONE`], otherwise overrides the
1301: ///   channel of the destination.
1302: ///
1303: /// Returns 0 on success, or 1 if the conversation, actor, or destination was
1304: /// missing, or if an auto hub ran out of choices.
1305: #[unsafe(no_mangle)]
1306: pub extern "C" fn force_position(ctx: *mut LouderContext, conversation: LouderName, actor: LouderActor, target_position: LouderPosition, fake_channel: LouderName, fake_priority: i32) -> i32 {
1307: if ctx.is_null() {
1308:   return 1;
1309: }
1310: 
1311: unsafe {

13: /// One queued ask, together with the verdict the host gave it.
14: ///
15: /// An ask stays queued after being answered so that the host can keep walking
16: /// the cache, but it stops accepting answers once `verdict` is set.
17: struct PendingAsk {
18:   ask: LouderAsk,
19:   verdict: Option<i32>,
20: }
21: 
22: /// Where `content` sits in the name table: the id it already has, or the id it
23: /// would take if it were added now.
24: ///
25: /// Ids are the hash of the text, probed forward on collision. Both halves of
26: /// looking a name up come from here, so that the id an intern lands on and the
27: /// id a later lookup finds cannot disagree.
28: fn probe_name(names: &LouderNameMap, content: &LouderNameContent) -> LouderContextResult {
29:   let mut hasher = DefaultHasher::new();
30:   content.bytes.hash(&mut hasher);
31: 
32:   let mut name = hasher.finish() as LouderName;
33:   let mut step = 1;
34:   while let Some(existing) = names.get(&name) {
35:     if existing == content {
36:       return LouderContextResult::Found(name);
37:     }
38:     name = name.wrapping_add(step);
39:     step += 1;
40:   }
41: 
42:   LouderContextResult::NotFound(name)
43: }
44: 
45: /// Assign an id to `text` and record it in `names`.
46: ///
47: /// Free-standing so that a context can be built with its ids already in place.
48: /// Interning only ever needs the name table, and giving it its own entry point
49: /// keeps that visible, instead of forcing the rest of a context to exist first
50: /// just so one field can be filled in after the fact.
51: fn intern_into(names: &mut LouderNameMap, text: &str) -> LouderName {
52:   let content = LouderNameContent::new(text);
53:   match probe_name(names, &content) {
54:     LouderContextResult::Found(name) => name,
55:     LouderContextResult::NotFound(name) => {
56:       names.insert(name, content);
57:       name
58:     }
59:   }
60: }
61: 
62: /// The opaque handle to the engine.
63: ///
64: /// Obtained from [`crate::init_louder`] and released with
65: /// [`crate::deinit_louder`]. The contents are internal to the engine and not
66: /// part of the public API.
67: ///
68: /// Story state is held here rather than on [`Conversation`] so that the shared
69: /// buckets survive loading and unloading individual conversations.
70: ///
71: /// The engine's whole vocabulary is reached through this one type: names,
72: /// conversations, actors, hubs, speakers, story state, the event queue, and the
73: /// ask cache.
74: pub struct LouderContext {
75:   names: LouderNameMap,
76:   main_channel: LouderName,
77:   rules: DialogueRules,
78:   speakers: Map<LouderName, Speaker>,
79:   conversations: Map<LouderName, Conversation>,
80:   actors: Map<LouderName, Vec<Actor>>,
81:   next_actor_id: Map<LouderName, LouderActor>,
82:   shared_saved: VariableMap,
83:   shared_unsaved: VariableMap,
84:   events: Vec<LouderEvent>,
85:   asks: VecDeque<PendingAsk>,
86: }
87:

89: impl LouderContext {
90:   pub fn new() -> LouderContext {
91:     let mut names = LouderNameMap::new();
92:     LouderContext {
93:       // Interned eagerly so that "main" always has an id, even in a
94:       // conversation that never mentions it.
95:       main_channel: intern_into(&mut names, LOUDER_MAIN_CHANNEL),
96:       names,
97:       // Every rule on, since each one is a default the guide describes rather
98:       // than an opt-in a conversation has to ask for.
99:       rules: DialogueRules::all(),
100:       speakers: Map::new(),
101:       conversations: Map::new(),
102:       actors: Map::new(),
103:       next_actor_id: Map::new(),

106:       events: Vec::new(),
107:       asks: VecDeque::new(),
108:     }
109:   }

124:   }

126:   pub fn push_event(&mut self, event: LouderEvent) -> usize {
127:     self.events.push(event);
128:     self.events.len() - 1
129:   }
130: 
131:   /// The queued event at `index`, if there is one.
132:   pub fn event(&self, index: usize) -> Option<&LouderEvent> {
133:     self.events.get(index)
134:   }
135:

139:   }
140: 
141:   pub fn clear_events(&mut self) {
142:     self.events.clear();
143:   }
144: 
145:   pub fn push_ask(&mut self, ask: LouderAsk) -> usize {
146:     self.asks.push_back(PendingAsk { ask, verdict: None });
147:     self.asks.len() - 1
148:   }
149: 
150:   /// The ask at `index`, or `None` if the index is out of range.
151:   pub fn ask(&self, index: usize) -> Option<&LouderAsk> {
152:     self.asks.get(index).map(|entry| &entry.ask)
153:   }
154: 
155:   /// How many asks are cached, answered or not.
156:   pub fn ask_count(&self) -> usize {
157:     self.asks.len()
158:   }

160:   /// Record `verdict` for the ask at `index` and move it to the back of the
161:   /// queue.
162:   ///
163:   /// Returns false when `index` is out of range or that ask was already
164:   /// answered. Answered asks remain readable so the host can keep polling the
165:   /// whole cache, but they stop accepting further answers.
166:   pub fn answer_ask(&mut self, index: usize, verdict: i32) -> bool {
167:     let Some(entry) = self.asks.get_mut(index) else {
168:       return false;
169:     };
170:     if entry.verdict.is_some() {
171:       return false;
172:     }
173:     entry.verdict = Some(verdict);
174: 
175:     // Answering moves the ask to the back of the queue, so that a host polling
176:     // the whole cache sees unanswered asks first.
177:     let answered = self.asks.remove(index).expect("the index was just found");
178:     self.asks.push_back(answered);
179:     true
180:   }

201:   /// Insert a conversation, reporting whether the name was already taken.

207:   /// Remove a conversation along with its actors, and announce its removal.

210:   pub fn remove_conversation(&mut self, name: LouderName) -> bool {
211:     self.actors.remove(&name);
212:     self.next_actor_id.remove(&name);
213: 
214:     if self.conversations.remove(&name).is_none() {
215:       return false;
216:     }
217: 
218:     self.push_event(LouderEvent {
219:       conversation: name,
220:       channel: LOUDER_NAME_NONE,
221:       actor: LOUDER_ACTOR_NONE,
222:       data: EventData::ConversationRemoved(name),
223:     });
224: 
225:     true
226:   }

229: 
230:   /// The actors of a conversation, in ascending id order.
231:   pub fn actors(&self, conversation: LouderName) -> &[Actor] {
232:     match self.actors.get(&conversation) {
233:       Some(actors) => actors,
234:       None => &[],
235:     }
236:   }
237: 
238:   pub fn get_actor(&self, conversation: LouderName, actor: LouderActor) -> Option<&Actor> {
239:     let actors = self.actors.get(&conversation)?;
240:     actors.iter().find(|a| a.id() == actor)
241:   }
242: 
243:   pub fn get_actor_mut(&mut self, conversation: LouderName, actor: LouderActor) -> Option<&mut Actor> {
244:     let actors = self.actors.get_mut(&conversation)?;
245:     actors.iter_mut().find(|a| a.id() == actor)
246:   }
247: 
248:   /// Append an actor, giving it the next unused id for its conversation, and
249:   /// announce it.
250:   ///
251:   /// Ids are never reused, so ascending id order is a stable ordering even
252:   /// after actors are removed. The event is emitted here rather than by the
253:   /// caller so that an actor cannot enter a conversation unannounced.
254:   pub fn add_actor(&mut self, conversation: LouderName, mut actor: Actor) -> LouderActor {
255:     let next = self.next_actor_id.entry(conversation).or_insert(0);
256:     let id = *next;
257:     *next += 1;
258:     actor.set_id(id);
259: 
260:     let channel = actor.channel();
261:     let actors = self.actors.entry(conversation).or_default();
262:     actors.push(actor);
263: 
264:     self.push_event(LouderEvent {
265:       conversation,
266:       channel,
267:       actor: id,
268:       data: EventData::ActorAdded,
269:     });
270: 
271:     id
272:   }
273: 
274:   /// Remove an actor, reporting whether it was there, and announce its removal.
275:   pub fn remove_actor(&mut self, conversation: LouderName, actor: LouderActor) -> bool {
276:     let removed = match self.actors.get_mut(&conversation) {
277:       Some(actors) => {
278:         let before = actors.len();
279:         actors.retain(|a| a.id() != actor);
280:         actors.len() != before
281:       }
282:       None => false,
283:     };
284: 
285:     if removed {
286:       self.push_event(LouderEvent {
287:         conversation,
288:         channel: LOUDER_NAME_NONE,
289:         actor,
290:         data: EventData::ActorRemoved,
291:       });
292:     }
293: 
294:     removed
295:   }

305:   pub fn add_speaker(&mut self, speaker: Speaker) -> bool {

309: 
310:   /// Insert a speaker, replacing any existing one with the same name.

313:     self.speakers.insert(name, speaker);
314:   }
315:

319:     names.sort_unstable();
320:     names
321:   }

323:   // -- story state --------------------------------------------------------
324: 
325:   fn variables_mut(&mut self, conversation: LouderName, scope: VariableScope) -> Option<&mut VariableMap> {
326:     match scope {
327:       VariableScope::LocalSaved | VariableScope::LocalUnsaved => {
328:         Some(self.conversations.get_mut(&conversation)?.variables_mut(scope))
329:       }
330:       VariableScope::SharedSaved => Some(&mut self.shared_saved),
331:       VariableScope::SharedUnsaved => Some(&mut self.shared_unsaved),
332:     }
333:   }
334: 
335:   fn variables(&self, conversation: LouderName, scope: VariableScope) -> Option<&VariableMap> {
336:     match scope {
337:       VariableScope::LocalSaved | VariableScope::LocalUnsaved => {
338:         self.conversations.get(&conversation).map(|c| c.variables(scope))
339:       }
340:       VariableScope::SharedSaved => Some(&self.shared_saved),
341:       VariableScope::SharedUnsaved => Some(&self.shared_unsaved),
342:     }
343:   }
344: 
345:   /// The scope a variable name falls into, read from its own text.
346:   pub fn scope_of(&self, key: LouderName) -> VariableScope {
347:     match self.resolve_name(key) {
348:       Some(text) => classify_variable(text),
349:       None => VariableScope::LocalSaved,
350:     }
351:   }
352: 
353:   /// Read a story state variable. Uninitialized variables read as 0.
354:   pub fn get_state(&self, conversation: LouderName, key: LouderName) -> LouderVariant {
355:     self
356:       .variables(conversation, self.scope_of(key))
357:       .and_then(|variables| variables.get(&key).cloned())
358:       .unwrap_or_default()
359:   }
360: 
361:   /// Write a story state variable, storing it in the bucket its name implies.
362:   ///
363:   /// Returns false when the variable is local and its conversation is not
364:   /// loaded, since there would be nowhere to put it. A shared variable always
365:   /// has somewhere to go.
366:   pub fn set_state(&mut self, conversation: LouderName, key: LouderName, value: LouderVariant) -> bool {
367:     match self.variables_mut(conversation, self.scope_of(key)) {
368:       Some(variables) => {
369:         variables.insert(key, value);
370:         true
371:       }
372:       None => false,
373:     }
374:   }
375: 
376:   /// Whether a story state variable has been written.
377:   pub fn has_state(&self, conversation: LouderName, key: LouderName) -> bool {
378:     self
379:       .variables(conversation, self.scope_of(key))
380:       .is_some_and(|variables| variables.contains_key(&key))
381:   }
382: 
383:   /// Every key written in the conversation, in ascending id order.
384:   ///
385:   /// Covers both local buckets.
386:   pub fn conversation_state_keys(&self, conversation: LouderName) -> Vec<LouderName> {
387:     let Some(conversation) = self.conversations.get(&conversation) else {
388:       return Vec::new();
389:     };
390: 
391:     let mut keys: Vec<LouderName> = conversation
392:       .variables(VariableScope::LocalSaved)
393:       .keys()
394:       .chain(conversation.variables(VariableScope::LocalUnsaved).keys())
395:       .copied()
396:       .collect();
397:     keys.sort_unstable();
398:     keys.dedup();
399:     keys
400:   }
401: 
402:   /// Every key written in either shared bucket, in ascending id order.
403:   pub fn shared_state_keys(&self) -> Vec<LouderName> {
404:     let mut keys: Vec<LouderName> = self.shared_saved.keys().chain(self.shared_unsaved.keys()).copied().collect();
405:     keys.sort_unstable();
406:     keys.dedup();
407:     keys
408:   }
409: 
410:   /// Every key written anywhere, local or shared, in ascending id order.
411:   ///
412:   /// This is what a save of the story is assembled from, so it lives here rather
413:   /// than being spelled out again at each place that saves.
414:   pub fn all_state_keys(&self, conversation: LouderName) -> Vec<LouderName> {
415:     let mut keys = self.conversation_state_keys(conversation);
416:     keys.extend(self.shared_state_keys());
417:     keys.sort_unstable();
418:     keys.dedup();
419:     keys
420:   }
421: }

26: /// An entity that speaks as and listens to speakers within a conversation.
27: ///
28: /// Its id is assigned by the context when it is added, since ids are per
29: /// conversation and the context is what hands them out.
30: ///
31: /// The actor a context hands out is a copy, so a host that wants one to move
32: /// says so through [`LouderContext`](super::context::LouderContext) rather
33: /// than by writing to the copy it was given.
34: #[derive(Clone)]

53:     // Spectators and debug actors are treated as having neither a channel nor a
54:     // priority, so they must not be given one at creation either.

133: impl ActorFlags {
134:   /// Whether an actor with these flags takes part in channels and priority.
135:   ///
136:   /// Spectators see every hub but are not in the flow, so the guide treats them
137:   /// as having no channel and no priority. This is the one place that rule is
138:   /// written down.
139:   pub fn in_flow(self) -> bool {
140:     !self.intersects(ActorFlags::SPECTATOR | ActorFlags::DEBUG)
141:   }

163:   /// whoever is standing on it. That is the projection the engine guide
164:   /// describes, and it is why the answer needs the actor as well as the hub.
165:   ///
166:   /// The hub itself is left alone: an anonymous hub stays anonymous, and the
167:   /// voice is projected onto it for as long as somebody is standing there.
168:   pub fn hub_speaker_for(
169:     context: &LouderContext,
170:     conversation: LouderName,
171:     actor: LouderActor,
172:     position: LouderPosition,
173:   ) -> LouderName {
174:     let Some(hub) = context.get_conversation(conversation).and_then(|c| c.hub(position)) else {
175:       return LOUDER_NAME_NONE;
176:     };
177: 
178:     let speak_as: Vec<LouderName> = context
179:       .get_actor(conversation, actor)
180:       .map(|entry| entry.speak_as().to_vec())
181:       .unwrap_or_default();
182: 
183:     hub.speaker_for(&speak_as)

190:   /// hub appears to speak as for that move alone, and the hub itself is left as
191:   /// it was, so the next actor to arrive gets the projection again.
192:   fn apparent_speaker(
193:     context: &mut LouderContext,
194:     conversation: LouderName,
195:     actor: LouderActor,
196:     position: LouderPosition,
197:   ) -> LouderName {
198:     let speaker = Self::hub_speaker_for(context, conversation, actor, position);
199: 
200:     let Some(hub) = context.get_conversation(conversation).and_then(|c| c.hub(position)) else {
201:       return speaker;
202:     };
203: 
204:     // The source text is copied out because evaluating it needs mutable access
205:     // to the same context that holds it.
206:     let sources: Vec<String> = hub
207:       .expressions()
208:       .iter()
209:       .filter_map(|expression| context.resolve_name(*expression).map(|source| source.to_string()))
210:       .collect();
211: 
212:     let channel = context
213:       .get_actor(conversation, actor)
214:       .map(|entry| entry.channel())
215:       .unwrap_or(LOUDER_NAME_NONE);
216: 
217:     let mut scope = EvaluationScope::new(conversation, actor, channel);
218:     scope.hub = position;
219: 
220:     for source in sources {
221:       let Ok(expr) = super::expression::parse(&source) else {
222:         continue;
223:       };
224: 
225:       let Some((name, value)) = super::expression::special_write(context, &scope, &expr) else {
226:         continue;
227:       };
228: 
229:       if name != super::expression::SPEAKER_VARIABLE {
230:         continue;
231:       }
232: 
233:       // A write that does not name a speaker changes nothing, which is what
234:       // keeps a hub with a broken write from losing its voice.
235:       if let LouderVariant::String(written) = value
236:         && written != LOUDER_NAME_NONE
237:       {
238:         return written;
239:       }
240:     }
241: 
242:     speaker
243:   }
244: }
