1197:         get_hub_choice_count(g_ctx, conversation, 26, actor_b));
1198:   CHECK(get_hub_choice_count(g_ctx, conversation, 27, actor_b) == 2,
1199:         "B should be offered both branches out of the auto hub, the private voice and the public "
1200:         "line, saw %d",
1201:         get_hub_choice_count(g_ctx, conversation, 27, actor_b));
1202:   CHECK(get_hub_choice_link(g_ctx, conversation, 27, actor_b, 0) == 29,
1203:         "B's first branch should be the private voice");
1204:   CHECK(get_hub_choice_link(g_ctx, conversation, 27, actor_b, 1) == 28,
1205:         "B's second branch should be the public line");
1206: 
1207:   // The other actor hears neither the private voice nor a claim on the public
1208:   // line, so neither branch is available to it and auto logic falls back to the
1209:   // public line for it. Emulation, not the leader's choice, is what gets it
1210:   // there.
1211:   CHECK(get_hub_choice_count(g_ctx, conversation, 27, actor_c) == 0,
1212:         "the other actor has no claim on either branch of the auto hub, saw %d",
1213:         get_hub_choice_count(g_ctx, conversation, 27, actor_c));
1214: 
1215:   // B chooses into the auto hub. B should resolve through it onto the private
1216:   // voice, 29, because that is the first branch available to B.
1217:   CHECK(select_choice(g_ctx, conversation, actor_b, 0) == 0,
1218:         "B's choice into the auto hub should be accepted");
1219:   CHECK(get_actor_position(g_ctx, conversation, actor_b) == 29,
1220:         "B should resolve through the auto hub onto the private voice it hears, saw %d",
1221:         get_actor_position(g_ctx, conversation, actor_b));
1222: 
1223:   // C is warped through the same auto hub and emulates it as itself. C's first
1224:   // available branch is none, so C takes the first branch that appears to it:
1225:   // the public line, 28. This is the divergence the demo's note describes.
1226:   CHECK(get_actor_position(g_ctx, conversation, actor_c) == 28,
1227:         "the other actor should diverge onto the public line, saw %d",
1228:         get_actor_position(g_ctx, conversation, actor_c));
1229: 
1230:   // The divergence is a warp, not a second choice, so a host can tell it apart
1231:   // from B leading.
1232:   std::vector<LouderEvent> events = DrainEvents();
1233:   CHECK(CountEventsOfKind(events, EventData::Tag::Warp) == 1,
1234:         "the other actor's divergence should be exactly one warp, saw %d",
1235:         CountEventsOfKind(events, EventData::Tag::Warp));
1236: 
1237:   remove_conversation(g_ctx, conversation);
1238:   clear_config(g_ctx);
1239: }
1240: 
1241: // The guide's Configuration section says the spoke rule and the singleplayer
1242: // rule can be turned on and off through the engine. Both are on in a fresh
1243: // context, and turning either off has to change what an actor is offered rather
1244: // than only what it can be told about.
1245: //
1246: // The spoke rule is what lets a lone choice out of a hub go ahead when the voice
1247: // behind it belongs to somebody the actor neither speaks nor hears. Whisper is
1248: // that voice here: hub 5 is spoken by it, nothing is public, and the actor
1249: // speaks only A. So the whole choice comes down to whether the rule is on.
1250: // The spoke rule is the one choice rule the guide does not qualify with "no
1251: // other actor has that speaker in its speak as list". Cases 2 and 3 are about a
1252: // voice nobody is playing; case 4 is about a hub with nowhere else to go. So a
1253: // fixture for it needs a voice that is held elsewhere and reachable anyway:
1254: //
1255: //   0: A (public)  ->  5: B (public)  ->  9: A
1256: //
1257: // B is public, so hub 5 appears to the A actor, but another actor speaks B, so
1258: // the second and third cases refuse it. Hub 0 has one choice, so the rule lets
1259: // it through anyway. Turn the rule off and the A actor is left standing on hub 0
1260: // with nothing, which is the dead end the rule exists to prevent.
1261: const char* SpokeRuleJson() {
1262:   return R"([
1263:     {"type":"hub","conversation_id":0,"speaker":"A","text":"ask","flags":0},
1264:     {"type":"hub","conversation_id":5,"speaker":"B","text":"B's line","flags":0},
1265:     {"type":"hub","conversation_id":9,"speaker":"A","text":"after","flags":0},
1266:     {"type":"connection","from_id":0,"from_port":0,"to_id":5},
1267:     {"type":"connection","from_id":5,"from_port":0,"to_id":9}
1268:   ])";
1269: }
1270: 
1271: // Both voices are public, so "reachable" is not what is being tested here. What
1272: // separates case 4 from cases 2 and 3 is the other actor holding B. Whisper is
1273: // declared and not public, since an undefined speaker is public by default and
1274: // would make the last block below meaningless.
1275: const char* SpokeRuleConfigJson() {
1276:   return R"([
1277:     {"type":"speaker","name":"A","flags":1},
1278:     {"type":"speaker","name":"B","flags":1},
1279:     {"type":"speaker","name":"Whisper","flags":0}
1280:   ])";
1281: }
1282: 
1283: void TestSpokeRuleIsConfigurable() {
1284:   std::printf("spoke rule configuration\n");
1285: 
1286:   clear_config(g_ctx);
1287:   CHECK(load_conversation_config(
1288:             g_ctx,
1289:             reinterpret_cast<uint8_t*>(const_cast<char*>(SpokeRuleConfigJson())),
1290:             std::strlen(SpokeRuleConfigJson())) == 0,
1291:         "the spoke rule config should load");
1292: 
1293:   LouderName conversation = Declare("spoke rule conversation");
1294:   std::string json = SpokeRuleJson();
1295:   CHECK(load_conversation_json(g_ctx, conversation, reinterpret_cast<uint8_t*>(&json[0]),
1296:                                json.size()) == 0,
1297:         "loading the spoke rule conversation returned %d", 0);
1298: 
1299:   LouderName speak_a[1] = {Lookup("A")};
1300:   LouderName speak_b[1] = {Lookup("B")};
1301: 
1302:   // Both actors are present throughout. Who is in the conversation is part of
1303:   // the answer: without a B actor there is no held voice, and the second and
1304:   // third cases would settle this without the rule at all.
1305:   LouderActor actor = add_actor(g_ctx, conversation, 0, ActorFlags(), speak_a, 1, nullptr, 0);
1306:   add_actor(g_ctx, conversation, 0, ActorFlags(), speak_b, 1, nullptr, 0);
1307:   CHECK(actor == 0, "the first actor should get id 0, got %d", actor);
1308: 
1309:   flush_events(g_ctx);
1310: 
1311:   // The premise, stated rather than assumed: hub 5 does appear to this actor,
1312:   // and it is the other actor who is playing B.
1313:   CHECK(get_hub_text(g_ctx, conversation, 5) != LOUDER_NAME_NONE,
1314:         "hub 5 should have loaded, or the premise is wrong");
1315:   CHECK(get_speaker_flags(g_ctx, Lookup("B")).bits == SpeakerFlags_PUBLIC.bits,
1316:         "B should be public here, or this would not be testing case 4");
1317:   CHECK(get_speaker_flags(g_ctx, Lookup("A")).bits == SpeakerFlags_PUBLIC.bits,
1318:         "A should be public here, or the A actor could not see hub 5 at all");
1319: 
1320:   // With the rule on, the lone choice out of hub 0 goes ahead even though B's
1321:   // voice is somebody else's.
1322:   WithRules(DialogueRules_SPOKE | DialogueRules_SINGLEPLAYER, [&] {
1323:     CHECK(get_hub_choice_count(g_ctx, conversation, 0, actor) == 1,
1324:           "the spoke rule should let the lone choice through, saw %d",
1325:           get_hub_choice_count(g_ctx, conversation, 0, actor));
1326:     CHECK(select_choice(g_ctx, conversation, actor, 0) == 0,
1327:           "and the actor should be able to take it");
1328:     CHECK(get_actor_position(g_ctx, conversation, actor) == 5,
1329:           "landing on the other actor's line, saw %d",
1330:           get_actor_position(g_ctx, conversation, actor));
1331:   });
1332: 
1333:   // Back to hub 0 for the half that says the rule is what opened it.
1334:   force_position(g_ctx, conversation, actor, 0, LOUDER_NAME_NONE, 0);
1335:   flush_events(g_ctx);
1336:   WithRules(DialogueRules_SINGLEPLAYER, [&] {
1337:     CHECK(get_hub_choice_count(g_ctx, conversation, 0, actor) == 0,
1338:           "without the spoke rule a held voice closes the choice, saw %d",
1339:           get_hub_choice_count(g_ctx, conversation, 0, actor));
1340:     CHECK(select_choice(g_ctx, conversation, actor, 0) != 0,
1341:           "and selecting it should be refused");
1342:     CHECK(get_actor_position(g_ctx, conversation, actor) == 0,
1343:           "a refused choice should leave the actor where it was, saw %d",
1344:           get_actor_position(g_ctx, conversation, actor));
1345:   });
1346: 
1347:   // Turning it back on restores the offer, which is what says the switch is
1348:   // read per choice rather than latched at load time.
1349:   WithRules(DialogueRules_SPOKE | DialogueRules_SINGLEPLAYER, [&] {
1350:     CHECK(get_hub_choice_count(g_ctx, conversation, 0, actor) == 1,
1351:           "turning the rule back on should restore the choice, saw %d",
1352:           get_hub_choice_count(g_ctx, conversation, 0, actor));
1353:   });
1354: 
1355:   // And off again is the same answer as it was the first time, which is what
1356:   // says the switch is a switch and not a one-shot.
1357:   WithRules(DialogueRules_SINGLEPLAYER, [&] {
1358:     CHECK(get_hub_choice_count(g_ctx, conversation, 0, actor) == 0,
1359:           "and turning it off again should close it again, saw %d",
1360:           get_hub_choice_count(g_ctx, conversation, 0, actor));
1361:   });
1362: 
1363:   // The rule needs the voice to be reachable. Whisper is neither public nor
1364:   // heard by this actor, so hub 5 below is somewhere the actor cannot see, and
1365:   // no rule may offer a choice into it.
1366:   {
1367:     LouderName private_hub = Declare("private hub conversation");
1368:     const char* document = R"([
1369:       {"type":"hub","conversation_id":0,"speaker":"A","text":"ask","flags":0},
1370:       {"type":"hub","conversation_id":5,"speaker":"Whisper","text":"whisper","flags":0},
1371:       {"type":"connection","from_id":0,"from_port":0,"to_id":5}
1372:     ])";
1373:     CHECK(load_conversation_json(g_ctx, private_hub,
1374:                                  reinterpret_cast<uint8_t*>(const_cast<char*>(document)),
1375:                                  std::strlen(document)) == 0,
1376:           "the unreachable conversation should load");
1377: 
1378:     LouderActor alone = add_actor(g_ctx, private_hub, 0, ActorFlags(), speak_a, 1, nullptr, 0);
1379:     WithRules(DialogueRules_SPOKE | DialogueRules_SINGLEPLAYER, [&] {
1380:       CHECK(get_hub_choice_count(g_ctx, private_hub, 0, alone) == 0,
1381:             "the spoke rule must not offer a choice into a hub the actor cannot see, saw %d",
1382:             get_hub_choice_count(g_ctx, private_hub, 0, alone));
1383:     });
1384:     remove_conversation(g_ctx, private_hub);
1385:   }
1386: 
1387:   // A null context is running no dialogue, so it has no rules to report and
1388:   // refuses to be given any.
1389:   CHECK(get_dialogue_rules(nullptr).bits == 0, "a null context should report no rules");
1390:   CHECK(set_dialogue_rules(nullptr, DialogueRules_SPOKE) != 0,
1391:         "a null context should refuse rules");
1392: 
1393:   // Bits the engine does not know about are dropped rather than refused, so a
1394:   // host carrying a configuration from a newer build still gets what it can use.
1395:   WithRules(DialogueRules{/* .bits = */ (int32_t)0x7FFFFFFF}, [&] {
1396:     CHECK(CurrentRules().bits == (DialogueRules_SPOKE | DialogueRules_SINGLEPLAYER).bits,
1397:           "unknown bits should be dropped, got %d", CurrentRules().bits);
1398:   });
1399: 
1400:   remove_conversation(g_ctx, conversation);
1401:   clear_config(g_ctx);
1402: }
1403: 
1404: // The singleplayer rule. Hub 5 is reached from hub 0, which branched, has one
1405: // way out of it, and is spoken by P, who is the only actor that can hear it. So
1406: // it resolves on its own, and the actor lands past it at hub 9 instead of
1407: // standing on it.
1408: //
1409: // Hub 6 is the control that keeps the rule honest: one way out, reached from the
1410: // same branching hub, but public, so the other actor can see it and the rule
1411: // does not apply. Without that check the rule would be indistinguishable from
1412: // "any hub with one choice resolves".
1413: const char* SingleplayerJson() {
1414:   return R"([
1415:     {"type":"hub","conversation_id":0,"speaker":"A","text":"ask","flags":0},
1416:     {"type":"hub","conversation_id":5,"speaker":"P","text":"aside","flags":0},
1417:     {"type":"hub","conversation_id":6,"speaker":"A","text":"shared line","flags":0},
1418:     {"type":"hub","conversation_id":9,"speaker":"P","text":"past the aside","flags":0},
1419:     {"type":"hub","conversation_id":10,"speaker":"A","text":"past the shared line","flags":0},
1420:     {"type":"connection","from_id":0,"from_port":0,"to_id":5},
1421:     {"type":"connection","from_id":0,"from_port":1,"to_id":6},
1422:     {"type":"connection","from_id":5,"from_port":0,"to_id":9},
1423:     {"type":"connection","from_id":6,"from_port":0,"to_id":10}
1424:   ])";
1425: }
1426: 
1427: // P is private and only P hears it. A is public, so hub 6 reaches both actors.
1428: const char* SingleplayerConfigJson() {
1429:   return R"([
1430:     {"type":"speaker","name":"A","flags":1},
1431:     {"type":"speaker","name":"P","flags":0}
1432:   ])";
1433: }
1434: 
1435: void TestSingleplayerRule() {
1436:   std::printf("singleplayer rule\n");
1437: 
1438:   clear_config(g_ctx);
1439:   CHECK(load_conversation_config(
1440:             g_ctx,
1441:             reinterpret_cast<uint8_t*>(const_cast<char*>(SingleplayerConfigJson())),
1442:             std::strlen(SingleplayerConfigJson())) == 0,
1443:         "the singleplayer config should load");
1444: 
1445:   LouderName conversation = Declare("singleplayer conversation");
1446:   std::string json = SingleplayerJson();
1447:   CHECK(load_conversation_json(g_ctx, conversation, reinterpret_cast<uint8_t*>(&json[0]),
1448:                                json.size()) == 0,
1449:         "loading the singleplayer conversation returned %d", 0);
1450: 
1451:   LouderName speak_a[1] = {Lookup("A")};
1452:   LouderName speak_p[1] = {Lookup("P")};
1453: 
1454:   // Both actors start at hub 0 so they share the main channel, which is what
1455:   // lets one of them drag the other along.
1456:   LouderActor actor_p = add_actor(g_ctx, conversation, 0, ActorFlags(), speak_p, 1, nullptr, 0);
1457:   LouderActor actor_a = add_actor(g_ctx, conversation, 0, ActorFlags(), speak_a, 1, nullptr, 0);
1458:   CHECK(actor_p == 0 && actor_a == 1, "the actors should be 0 and 1, got %d and %d", actor_p,
1459:         actor_a);
1460: 
1461:   flush_events(g_ctx);
1462: 
1463:   // P is offered the branch into its own private hub, and not the public one,
1464:   // because A speaks A and the public hub belongs to A's voice.
1465:   CHECK(get_hub_choice_count(g_ctx, conversation, 0, actor_p) == 1,
1466:         "P should be offered the single choice into its own aside, saw %d",
1467:         get_hub_choice_count(g_ctx, conversation, 0, actor_p));
1468:   CHECK(get_hub_choice_link(g_ctx, conversation, 0, actor_p, 0) == 5,
1469:         "and that choice should lead to hub 5");
1470: 
1471:   // With the rule on, choosing into hub 5 does not stop there. Hub 5 is one way
1472:   // out, came from a hub that had two, and only P can see it, so it resolves.
1473:   WithRules(DialogueRules_SPOKE | DialogueRules_SINGLEPLAYER, [&] {
1474:     CHECK(select_choice(g_ctx, conversation, actor_p, 0) == 0,
1475:           "the choice into the aside should be accepted");
1476:     CHECK(get_actor_position(g_ctx, conversation, actor_p) == 9,
1477:           "the aside should have resolved on its own and carried P to hub 9, saw %d",
1478:           get_actor_position(g_ctx, conversation, actor_p));
1479:   });
1480: 
1481:   // A is not dragged onto hub 5 by P leaving it: the aside is private to P, so
1482:   // there is nothing about it A could be carried through to.
1483:   CHECK(get_actor_position(g_ctx, conversation, actor_a) == 0,
1484:         "the other actor should not have followed into a private hub, saw %d",
1485:         get_actor_position(g_ctx, conversation, actor_a));
1486: 
1487:   // The control: the same shape, but public, so the rule cannot claim it. A
1488:   // speaks A, and P's voice is held by P, so A is offered the public branch at
1489:   // engine index 1 rather than the one at index 0.
1490:   WithRules(DialogueRules_SPOKE | DialogueRules_SINGLEPLAYER, [&] {
1491:     CHECK(get_hub_choice_count(g_ctx, conversation, 0, actor_a) == 1,
1492:           "A should be offered just the public branch, saw %d",
1493:           get_hub_choice_count(g_ctx, conversation, 0, actor_a));
1494:     CHECK(get_hub_choice_link(g_ctx, conversation, 0, actor_a, 1) == 6,
1495:           "and it should be the one at engine index 1");
1496: 
1497:     CHECK(select_choice(g_ctx, conversation, actor_a, 1) == 0,
1498:           "A should be able to take the public branch");
1499:     CHECK(get_actor_position(g_ctx, conversation, actor_a) == 6,
1500:           "a public hub with one choice is an ordinary hub and should be stood on, saw %d",
1501:           get_actor_position(g_ctx, conversation, actor_a));
1502:   });
1503: 
1504:   remove_conversation(g_ctx, conversation);
1505: 
1506:   // The same conversation again with the rule off, which is the half that says
1507:   // the rule is what did it. A fresh conversation keeps the first one out of the
1508:   // way without having to reason about where anybody ended up.
1509:   LouderName without = Declare("singleplayer conversation, rule off");
1510:   CHECK(load_conversation_json(g_ctx, without, reinterpret_cast<uint8_t*>(&json[0]), json.size()) == 0,
1511:         "the second singleplayer conversation should load");
1512: 
1513:   LouderActor plain_p = add_actor(g_ctx, without, 0, ActorFlags(), speak_p, 1, nullptr, 0);
1514:   CHECK(plain_p == 0, "the second conversation's actor should get id 0");
1515: 
1516:   flush_events(g_ctx);
1517: 
1518:   WithRules(DialogueRules_SPOKE, [&] {
1519:     CHECK(select_choice(g_ctx, without, plain_p, 0) == 0,
1520:           "without the singleplayer rule the choice into the aside is still ordinary, and accepted");
1521:     CHECK(get_actor_position(g_ctx, without, plain_p) == 5,
1522:           "the actor should have stopped on the aside, saw %d",
1523:           get_actor_position(g_ctx, without, plain_p));
1524:     CHECK(get_hub_choice_count(g_ctx, without, 5, plain_p) == 1,
1525:           "and been offered its one way out, saw %d",
1526:           get_hub_choice_count(g_ctx, without, 5, plain_p));
1527:   });
1528: 
1529:   remove_conversation(g_ctx, without);
1530:   clear_config(g_ctx);
1531: }
1532: 
1533: // A load error is reported through the event queue rather than by failing, so
1534: // that one bad record does not cost the caller the rest of the document. The
1535: // whole fixture below is full of them, and the conversation still loads.
1536: //
1537: // Three kinds are here, because the position they report is what tells them
1538: // apart: a record that is not an object and so names no position at all, a
1539: // record of a type the loader does not know, and a connection naming a hub the
1540: // document never defines. The camera record is the control: it is ignored
1541: // outright and must not be reported at all.
1542: const char* BadRecordsJson() {
1543:   return R"([
1544:     {"type":"hub","conversation_id":0,"speaker":"A","text":"start","flags":0},
1545:     {"type":"hub","conversation_id":7,"speaker":"A","text":"good","flags":0},
1546:     "this record is not an object at all",
1547:     {"type":"mystery","conversation_id":1},
1548:     {"type":"connection","from_id":0,"from_port":0,"to_id":99},
1549:     {"type":"camera","position:x":"0.0","position:y":"0.0","zoom":"1.0"}
1550:   ])";
1551: }
1552: 
1553: void TestLoadErrorsAreReportedAsEvents() {
1554:   std::printf("load errors as events\n");
1555: 
1556:   LouderName conversation = Declare("bad records conversation");
1557:   std::string json = BadRecordsJson();
1558:   CHECK(load_conversation_json(g_ctx, conversation, reinterpret_cast<uint8_t*>(&json[0]),
1559:                                json.size()) == 0,
1560:         "a document with unreadable records should still load, got %d", 0);
1561: 
1562:   // Both hubs made it, which is the point of reporting rather than failing. Hub
1563:   // 0's connection pointed at a hub that does not exist, so it lost its choice
1564:   // but kept everything else.
1565:   CHECK(get_hub_text(g_ctx, conversation, 0) != LOUDER_NAME_NONE, "hub 0 should have loaded");
1566:   CHECK(get_hub_choice_count(g_ctx, conversation, 0, 0) == 0,
1567:         "hub 0's choice pointed at a hub that is not there, so it should have been dropped");
1568:   CHECK(get_hub_text(g_ctx, conversation, 7) != LOUDER_NAME_NONE, "hub 7 should have loaded");
1569: 
1570:   std::vector<LoadFailure> failures = DrainLoadErrors();
1571:   CHECK(failures.size() == 3,
1572:         "three records were unreadable, saw %zu", failures.size());
1573: 
1574:   // A record that is not an object has no position to point at, which is what
1575:   // the -1 means: the document as a whole is at fault rather than a hub in it.
1576:   if (failures.size() == 3) {
1577:     CHECK(failures[0].position == -1,
1578:           "a record that is not an object should report no position, got %d",
1579:           failures[0].position);
1580:     CHECK(failures[0].error == LOUDER_LOAD_NOT_AN_OBJECT,
1581:           "and should report that it was not an object, got %d", failures[0].error);
1582: 
1583:     // A record of an unknown type names the position it claimed, so a host can
1584:     // go and look at it in the editor.
1585:     CHECK(failures[1].position == 1, "an unknown record should report its own position, got %d",
1586:           failures[1].position);
1587:     CHECK(failures[1].error == LOUDER_LOAD_NOT_AN_OBJECT, "and should report the same error, got %d",
1588:           failures[1].error);
1589: 
1590:     // A connection to a hub that is not there is blamed on the hub it leaves,
1591:     // since that is the one a host can go and fix.
1592:     CHECK(failures[2].position == 0,
1593:           "a connection to a missing hub should be blamed on its source, got %d",
1594:           failures[2].position);
1595:     CHECK(failures[2].error == LOUDER_LOAD_NOT_AN_OBJECT, "and should report the same error, got %d",
1596:           failures[2].error);
1597:   }
1598: 
1599:   // A document that is not JSON at all is a problem with the document rather
1600:   // than with a record in it, and is reported the same way before the load is
1601:   // refused.
1602:   flush_events(g_ctx);
1603:   const char* garbage = "not json at all";
1604:   CHECK(load_conversation_json(g_ctx, Declare("garbage conversation"),
1605:                                reinterpret_cast<uint8_t*>(const_cast<char*>(garbage)),
1606:                                std::strlen(garbage)) != 0,
1607:         "malformed JSON should fail to load");
1608:   failures = DrainLoadErrors();
1609:   CHECK(failures.size() == 1, "a malformed document should be reported once, saw %zu",
1610:         failures.size());
1611:   if (failures.size() == 1) {
1612:     CHECK(failures[0].position == -1,
1613:           "a problem with the document as a whole should report no position, got %d",
1614:           failures[0].position);
1615:     CHECK(failures[0].error == LOUDER_LOAD_MALFORMED_JSON,
1616:           "and should say the document was malformed, got %d", failures[0].error);
1617:   }
1618: 
1619:   // A document that parses but is not a list has no record to point at either.
1620:   flush_events(g_ctx);
1621:   const char* not_a_list = R"({"type":"hub","conversation_id":0})";
1622:   CHECK(load_conversation_json(g_ctx, Declare("object conversation"),
1623:                                reinterpret_cast<uint8_t*>(const_cast<char*>(not_a_list)),
1624:                                std::strlen(not_a_list)) == LOUDER_LOAD_NOT_A_LIST,
1625:         "a document that is not a list should be refused");
1626:   failures = DrainLoadErrors();
1627:   CHECK(failures.size() == 1, "a document that is not a list should be reported once, saw %zu",
1628:         failures.size());
1629:   if (failures.size() == 1) {
1630:     CHECK(failures[0].position == -1, "and should report no position, got %d",
1631:           failures[0].position);
1632:     CHECK(failures[0].error == LOUDER_LOAD_NOT_A_LIST, "and should say so, got %d",
1633:           failures[0].error);
1634:   }
1635: 
1636:   remove_conversation(g_ctx, conversation);
1637: }
1638: 
1639: // The config loader reports the same way. It used to give up on the first
1640: // record it could not read, which cost the caller every speaker after it.
1641: void TestConfigLoadErrorsAreReportedAsEvents() {
1642:   std::printf("config load errors as events\n");
1643: 
1644:   clear_config(g_ctx);
1645:   flush_events(g_ctx);
1646: 
1647:   const char* config = R"([
1648:     {"type":"speaker","name":"Before","flags":1},
1649:     "this record is not an object at all",
1650:     {"type":"speaker","name":"After","flags":0}
1651:   ])";
1652: 
1653:   CHECK(load_conversation_config(g_ctx,
1654:                                  reinterpret_cast<uint8_t*>(const_cast<char*>(config)),
1655:                                  std::strlen(config)) == 0,
1656:         "a config with one bad record should still load");
1657: 
1658:   // The point of the change: the speakers on either side of the bad record both
1659:   // made it, which is what "does not cost the caller the rest" means.
1660:   CHECK(get_speaker_flags(g_ctx, Declare("Before")).bits == SpeakerFlags_PUBLIC.bits,
1661:         "the speaker before the bad record should have loaded");
1662:   CHECK(get_speaker_flags(g_ctx, Declare("After")).bits == 0,
1663:         "the speaker after the bad record should have loaded too");
1664: 
1665:   std::vector<LoadFailure> failures = DrainLoadErrors();
1666:   CHECK(failures.size() == 1, "the bad record should be reported once, saw %zu", failures.size());
1667:   if (failures.size() == 1) {
1668:     CHECK(failures[0].position == -1,
1669:           "a config record has no position, so it should report the document, got %d",
1670:           failures[0].position);
1671:     CHECK(failures[0].error == LOUDER_LOAD_NOT_AN_OBJECT, "and should say what was wrong, got %d",
1672:           failures[0].error);
1673:   }
1674: 
1675:   // A config that is not JSON is still refused, and still reported.
1676:   flush_events(g_ctx);
1677:   const char* garbage = "not json at all";
1678:   CHECK(load_conversation_config(g_ctx,
1679:                                  reinterpret_cast<uint8_t*>(const_cast<char*>(garbage)),
1680:                                  std::strlen(garbage)) != 0,
1681:         "a malformed config should fail to load");
1682:   failures = DrainLoadErrors();
1683:   CHECK(failures.size() == 1, "a malformed config should be reported once, saw %zu",
1684:         failures.size());
1685:   if (failures.size() == 1) {
1686:     CHECK(failures[0].error == LOUDER_LOAD_MALFORMED_JSON,
1687:           "and should say the document was malformed, got %d", failures[0].error);
1688:   }
1689: 
1690:   clear_config(g_ctx);
1691: }
1692: 
1693: // The shape the demo conversation runs into, reproduced without the demo.
1694: //
1695: // Benjamin stands at a hub that branches, makes a real choice out of it, and
1696: // lands on a hub only he can see because it carries him as a recipient. That is
1697: // the singleplayer rule's premise exactly: a choice was just made, and what it
1698: // led to is his alone, so it is the consequence of that choice rather than
1699: // another one, and it resolves on its own.
1700: //
1701: //   0: Freya, branches -> 10 (his aside)  and  20 (his thought)
1702: //   10: -> 30 (Memory, his private voice)  and  40 (Benjamin, public)
1703: //   20: -> 50
1704: //
1705: // Hub 10 has two choices and hub 20 has one, and the rule treats them the same,
1706: // because how many choices a hub carries is not what makes it a consequence.
1707: // What the rule does need is somewhere else to see it, which hub 60 below is
1708: // for: the same shape, but public, so it belongs to both actors and neither of
1709: // them may be marched through it on the other's say-so.
1710: //
1711: // The second actor is not a bystander here. It is what makes "only he can see
1712: // it" mean something, since an unopposed private hub is private to nobody in
1713: // particular.
1714: const char* PrivateBranchJson() {
1715:   return R"([
1716:     {"type":"hub","conversation_id":0,"speaker":"Freya","text":"ask","tags":"start","flags":0},
1717:     {"type":"hub","conversation_id":10,"speaker":"Benjamin","text":"his aside","recipients":"Benjamin","flags":0},
1718:     {"type":"hub","conversation_id":20,"speaker":"Benjamin","text":"his thought","recipients":"Benjamin","flags":0},
1719:     {"type":"hub","conversation_id":30,"speaker":"Memory","text":"what he remembers","flags":0},
1720:     {"type":"hub","conversation_id":40,"speaker":"Benjamin","text":"what he says","flags":0},
1721:     {"type":"hub","conversation_id":50,"speaker":"Benjamin","text":"past the thought","flags":0},
1722:     {"type":"hub","conversation_id":60,"speaker":"Benjamin","text":"a line they share","flags":0},
1723:     {"type":"hub","conversation_id":70,"speaker":"Freya","text":"the reply","flags":0},
1724:     {"type":"connection","from_id":0,"from_port":0,"to_id":20},
1725:     {"type":"connection","from_id":0,"from_port":1,"to_id":10},
1726:     {"type":"connection","from_id":0,"from_port":2,"to_id":60},
1727:     {"type":"connection","from_id":10,"from_port":0,"to_id":30},
1728:     {"type":"connection","from_id":10,"from_port":1,"to_id":40},
1729:     {"type":"connection","from_id":20,"from_port":0,"to_id":50},
1730:     {"type":"connection","from_id":60,"from_port":0,"to_id":70}
1731:   ])";
1732: }
1733: 
1734: // Benjamin hears Memory, so hub 30 reaches him and nothing else. Freya hears
1735: // neither, which is what keeps the private hubs off her screen while hub 60,
1736: // which is public, stays on it.
1737: const char* PrivateBranchConfigJson() {
1738:   return R"([
1739:     {"type":"speaker","name":"Freya","flags":1},
1740:     {"type":"speaker","name":"Benjamin","flags":1,"listening":"Memory"},
1741:     {"type":"speaker","name":"Memory","flags":0}
1742:   ])";
1743: }
1744: 
1745: void TestAPrivateHubReachedByAMeaningfulChoice() {
1746:   std::printf("a private hub reached by a meaningful choice\n");
1747: 
1748:   clear_config(g_ctx);
1749:   CHECK(load_conversation_config(
1750:             g_ctx,
1751:             reinterpret_cast<uint8_t*>(const_cast<char*>(PrivateBranchConfigJson())),
1752:             std::strlen(PrivateBranchConfigJson())) == 0,
1753:         "the private branch config should load");
1754: 
1755:   LouderName conversation = Declare("private branch conversation");
1756:   std::string json = PrivateBranchJson();
1757:   CHECK(load_conversation_json(g_ctx, conversation, reinterpret_cast<uint8_t*>(&json[0]),
1758:                                json.size()) == 0,
1759:         "loading the private branch conversation returned %d", 0);
1760: 
1761:   LouderName speak_freya[1] = {Lookup("Freya")};
1762:   LouderName speak_benjamin[1] = {Lookup("Benjamin")};
1763: 
1764:   LouderActor actor_benjamin =
1765:       add_actor(g_ctx, conversation, 0, ActorFlags(), speak_benjamin, 1, nullptr, 0);
1766:   LouderActor actor_freya = add_actor(g_ctx, conversation, 0, ActorFlags(), speak_freya, 1, nullptr, 0);
1767:   CHECK(actor_benjamin == 0 && actor_freya == 1, "the actors should be 0 and 1, got %d and %d",
1768:         actor_benjamin, actor_freya);
1769: 
1770:   flush_events(g_ctx);
1771: 
1772:   // Hub 10 is the case. It carries two choices and none of them is hidden from
1773:   // him, so he could perfectly well decide between them, and he is offered
1774:   // both. What settles it is not the count but that the hub is addressed to him
1775:   // alone and stands where a choice he just made put it.
1776:   WithRules(DialogueRules_SPOKE | DialogueRules_SINGLEPLAYER, [&] {
1777:     CHECK(get_hub_choice_count(g_ctx, conversation, 0, actor_benjamin) == 3,
1778:           "he should be offered all three of the branches, saw %d",
1779:           get_hub_choice_count(g_ctx, conversation, 0, actor_benjamin));
1780:     CHECK(get_hub_choice_link(g_ctx, conversation, 0, actor_benjamin, 1) == 10,
1781:           "the aside should be at engine index 1");
1782:     CHECK(get_hub_choice_count(g_ctx, conversation, 10, actor_benjamin) == 2,
1783:           "and both of the aside's own choices should be his to look at, saw %d",
1784:           get_hub_choice_count(g_ctx, conversation, 10, actor_benjamin));
1785: 
1786:     // Choosing into it does not stop there. He is resolved through on the first
1787:     // branch available to him, which is the voice only he hears.
1788:     CHECK(select_choice(g_ctx, conversation, actor_benjamin, 1) == 0,
1789:           "the choice into his aside should be accepted");
1790:     CHECK(get_actor_position(g_ctx, conversation, actor_benjamin) == 30,
1791:           "the aside should have resolved on its own, carrying him to the branch he alone hears at "
1792:           "hub 30, saw %d", get_actor_position(g_ctx, conversation, actor_benjamin));
1793:   });
1794: 
1795:   // Freya had no claim on either branch of hub 10, since it is addressed to him.
1796:   // But she is not deciding anything either, so she is emulated through it and
1797:   // takes the first branch that appears to her, which is hub 40 and not the hub
1798:   // 30 she cannot hear. Being carried is not the same as being able to choose,
1799:   // and this is where the two actors come apart.
1800:   CHECK(get_actor_position(g_ctx, conversation, actor_freya) == 40,
1801:         "Freya should have been carried through the aside onto the branch she can hear, saw %d",
1802:         get_actor_position(g_ctx, conversation, actor_freya));
1803: 
1804:   // Hub 20 has one choice rather than two, and resolves the same way. The count
1805:   // is not what does it, which is why both are here.
1806:   force_position(g_ctx, conversation, actor_benjamin, 0, LOUDER_NAME_NONE, 0);
1807:   force_position(g_ctx, conversation, actor_freya, 0, LOUDER_NAME_NONE, 0);
1808:   flush_events(g_ctx);
1809:   WithRules(DialogueRules_SPOKE | DialogueRules_SINGLEPLAYER, [&] {
1810:     CHECK(select_choice(g_ctx, conversation, actor_benjamin, 0) == 0,
1811:           "the choice into his thought should be accepted");
1812:     CHECK(get_actor_position(g_ctx, conversation, actor_benjamin) == 50,
1813:           "one choice out of a hub that branched resolves on its own too, carrying him to hub 50, "
1814:           "saw %d", get_actor_position(g_ctx, conversation, actor_benjamin));
1815:   });
1816: 
1817:   // Hub 60 is the control. Same shape, same single choice, reached from the same
1818:   // branching hub, but public: both actors can see it, so it belongs to both of
1819:   // them and the rule cannot claim it on one of their say-so. Without this the
1820:   // rule would just be "any hub an actor walks into resolves".
1821:   force_position(g_ctx, conversation, actor_benjamin, 0, LOUDER_NAME_NONE, 0);
1822:   force_position(g_ctx, conversation, actor_freya, 0, LOUDER_NAME_NONE, 0);
1823:   flush_events(g_ctx);
1824:   WithRules(DialogueRules_SPOKE | DialogueRules_SINGLEPLAYER, [&] {
1825:     CHECK(select_choice(g_ctx, conversation, actor_benjamin, 2) == 0,
1826:           "the shared line should be an ordinary choice");
1827:     CHECK(get_actor_position(g_ctx, conversation, actor_benjamin) == 60,
1828:           "and he should stand on it, since the other actor can see it too, saw %d",
1829:           get_actor_position(g_ctx, conversation, actor_benjamin));
1830:   });
1831: 
1832:   // The one thing that would make the rule worth having: with it off, the same
1833:   // move stops where the player was sent.
1834:   force_position(g_ctx, conversation, actor_benjamin, 0, LOUDER_NAME_NONE, 0);
1835:   force_position(g_ctx, conversation, actor_freya, 0, LOUDER_NAME_NONE, 0);
1836:   flush_events(g_ctx);
1837:   WithRules(DialogueRules_SPOKE, [&] {
1838:     CHECK(select_choice(g_ctx, conversation, actor_benjamin, 1) == 0,
1839:           "without the singleplayer rule the choice into his aside is ordinary, and accepted");
1840:     CHECK(get_actor_position(g_ctx, conversation, actor_benjamin) == 10,
1841:           "he should have stopped on it, since it is a decision he could have made, saw %d",
1842:           get_actor_position(g_ctx, conversation, actor_benjamin));
1843:     CHECK(get_hub_choice_count(g_ctx, conversation, 10, actor_benjamin) == 2,
1844:           "and been offered both of its branches, saw %d",
1845:           get_hub_choice_count(g_ctx, conversation, 10, actor_benjamin));
1846:   });
1847: 
1848:   remove_conversation(g_ctx, conversation);
1849:   clear_config(g_ctx);
1850: }
1851: 
1852: // The other demo case. An interface shows a side with exactly one choice as
1853: // CONTINUE, so both sides have to be able to be in that state at the same hub.
1854: // What makes that possible is a hub nobody owns: it has no speaker and no
1855: // recipients, so it reaches every actor, and it is held by nobody, so ownership
1856: // never comes into it.
1857: //
1858: //   0: -> 60 -> 70
1859: //
1860: // Hub 60 is the one both actors stand on to be "continuing".
1861: const char* ContinueJson() {
1862:   return R"([
1863:     {"type":"hub","conversation_id":0,"speaker":"A","text":"start","tags":"start","flags":0},
1864:     {"type":"hub","conversation_id":60,"text":"","flags":0},
1865:     {"type":"hub","conversation_id":70,"text":"the end","flags":0},
1866:     {"type":"connection","from_id":0,"from_port":0,"to_id":60},
1867:     {"type":"connection","from_id":60,"from_port":0,"to_id":70}
1868:   ])";
1869: }
1870: 
1871: void TestAnUnownedHubWithOneChoiceIsEveryonesToTake() {
1872:   std::printf("an unowned hub with one choice is everyone's to take\n");
1873: 
1874:   LouderName conversation = Declare("continue conversation");
1875:   std::string json = ContinueJson();
1876:   CHECK(load_conversation_json(g_ctx, conversation, reinterpret_cast<uint8_t*>(&json[0]),
1877:                                json.size()) == 0,
1878:         "loading the continue conversation returned %d", 0);
1879: 
1880:   LouderName speak_a[1] = {Lookup("A")};
1881:   LouderName speak_b[1] = {Lookup("B")};
1882:   LouderActor actor_a = add_actor(g_ctx, conversation, 60, ActorFlags(), speak_a, 1, nullptr, 0);
1883:   LouderActor actor_b = add_actor(g_ctx, conversation, 60, ActorFlags(), speak_b, 1, nullptr, 0);
1884:   CHECK(actor_a == 0 && actor_b == 1, "the actors should be 0 and 1, got %d and %d", actor_a,
1885:         actor_b);
1886: 
1887:   flush_events(g_ctx);
1888: 
1889:   // Hub 60 has no speaker and no recipients, so it reaches both actors and belongs
1890:   // to neither. That is the whole premise of a CONTINUE: two sides that are both
1891:   // showing one choice and neither of them is the other one's to take away.
1892:   CHECK(get_hub_speaker(g_ctx, conversation, 60) == LOUDER_NAME_NONE,
1893:         "hub 60 should carry no speaker, which is what leaves it unowned");
1894:   CHECK(get_hub_choice_count(g_ctx, conversation, 60, actor_a) == 1,
1895:         "the first actor should see exactly one choice, saw %d",
1896:         get_hub_choice_count(g_ctx, conversation, 60, actor_a));
1897:   CHECK(get_hub_choice_count(g_ctx, conversation, 60, actor_b) == 1,
1898:         "and so should the second, which is what puts both sides in that state, saw %d",
1899:         get_hub_choice_count(g_ctx, conversation, 60, actor_b));
1900:   CHECK(get_hub_choice_link(g_ctx, conversation, 60, actor_a, 0) == 70,
1901:         "the choice should lead to hub 70");
1902:   CHECK(get_hub_choice_link(g_ctx, conversation, 60, actor_b, 0) == 70,
1903:         "and to the same place for both, since neither of them chose for the other");
1904: 
1905:   // Each may take it for itself. What that does not mean is that one side
1906:   // decides for the other: the other is carried, not commanded, and only
1907:   // because the two share a channel and hub 70 is visible to both. A side on its
1908:   // own channel would simply stay put, which is the case that keeps a CONTINUE
1909:   // from being mistaken for one player moving the other.
1910:   WithRules(DialogueRules_SPOKE | DialogueRules_SINGLEPLAYER, [&] {
1911:     CHECK(select_choice(g_ctx, conversation, actor_a, 0) == 0,
1912:           "the first actor should be able to continue");
1913:     CHECK(get_actor_position(g_ctx, conversation, actor_a) == 70,
1914:         "and should land on hub 70, saw %d", get_actor_position(g_ctx, conversation, actor_a));
1915:     CHECK(get_actor_position(g_ctx, conversation, actor_b) == 70,
1916:           "and the second should follow it there, since they share a channel and can both see hub "
1917:           "70, saw %d", get_actor_position(g_ctx, conversation, actor_b));
1918:   });
1919: 
1920:   // Put on separate channels and neither moves the other, which is what makes
1921:   // the choice each side's rather than the conversation's.
1922:   force_position(g_ctx, conversation, actor_a, 60, Declare("left"), 0);
1923:   force_position(g_ctx, conversation, actor_b, 60, Declare("right"), 0);
1924:   flush_events(g_ctx);
1925:   WithRules(DialogueRules_SPOKE | DialogueRules_SINGLEPLAYER, [&] {
1926:     CHECK(select_choice(g_ctx, conversation, actor_a, 0) == 0,
1927:           "the first actor should still be able to continue");
1928:     CHECK(get_actor_position(g_ctx, conversation, actor_a) == 70,
1929:           "landing on hub 70, saw %d", get_actor_position(g_ctx, conversation, actor_a));
1930:     CHECK(get_actor_position(g_ctx, conversation, actor_b) == 60,
1931:           "while the other side, in another channel, is left to continue on its own, saw %d",
1932:           get_actor_position(g_ctx, conversation, actor_b));
1933:     CHECK(get_hub_choice_count(g_ctx, conversation, 60, actor_b) == 1,
1934:           "and is still offered its one way on, saw %d",
1935:           get_hub_choice_count(g_ctx, conversation, 60, actor_b));
1936:   });
1937: 
1938:   // And it is a genuine choice rather than something the engine pushes through:
1939:   // with the singleplayer rule off it is still offered, which is the difference
1940:   // between "both sides may continue" and "both sides are continuing".
1941:   force_position(g_ctx, conversation, actor_a, 60, LOUDER_NAME_NONE, 0);
1942:   force_position(g_ctx, conversation, actor_b, 60, LOUDER_NAME_NONE, 0);
1943:   WithRules(DialogueRules_SPOKE, [&] {
1944:     CHECK(get_hub_choice_count(g_ctx, conversation, 60, actor_a) == 1,
1945:           "an unowned hub is not the singleplayer rule's business, and stays a choice, saw %d",
1946:           get_hub_choice_count(g_ctx, conversation, 60, actor_a));
1947:     CHECK(get_hub_choice_count(g_ctx, conversation, 60, actor_b) == 1,
1948:           "on both sides, saw %d", get_hub_choice_count(g_ctx, conversation, 60, actor_b));
1949:   });
1950: 
1951:   remove_conversation(g_ctx, conversation);
1952: }
1953: 
1954: // A hub with no speaker of its own is anonymous: it says whatever whoever is
1955: // standing on it says, and the voice is projected onto the hub for as long as
1956: // that actor is there.
1957: //
1958: //   0 -> 5 -> 9
1959: //
1960: // Every hub here is anonymous, so the conversation does not depend on who is
1961: // public: an anonymous hub is reached by everybody and owned by nobody.
1962: const char* AnonymousJson() {
1963:   return R"([
1964:     {"type":"hub","conversation_id":0,"text":"start","tags":"start","flags":0},
1965:     {"type":"hub","conversation_id":5,"text":"anonymous","flags":0},
1966:     {"type":"hub","conversation_id":9,"text":"the end","flags":0},
1967:     {"type":"connection","from_id":0,"from_port":0,"to_id":5},
1968:     {"type":"connection","from_id":5,"from_port":0,"to_id":9}
1969:   ])";
1970: }
1971: 
1972: // The same conversation, except that hub 5 asks who would be speaking it and
1973: // turns itself away from that actor.
1974: const char* GatedJson() {
1975:   return R"([
1976:     {"type":"hub","conversation_id":0,"text":"start","tags":"start","flags":0},
1977:     {"type":"hub","conversation_id":5,"text":"gated","expressions":"%speaker != \"A\"","flags":0},
1978:     {"type":"hub","conversation_id":9,"text":"the end","flags":0},
1979:     {"type":"connection","from_id":0,"from_port":0,"to_id":5},
1980:     {"type":"connection","from_id":5,"from_port":0,"to_id":9}
1981:   ])";
1982: }
1983: 
1984: // The same conversation again, except that hub 5 remembers who would be
1985: // speaking it.
1986: const char* RememberedJson() {
1987:   return R"([
1988:     {"type":"hub","conversation_id":0,"text":"start","tags":"start","flags":0},
1989:     {"type":"hub","conversation_id":5,"text":"remembered","expressions":"last = %speaker","flags":0},
1990:     {"type":"hub","conversation_id":9,"text":"the end","flags":0},
1991:     {"type":"connection","from_id":0,"from_port":0,"to_id":5},
1992:     {"type":"connection","from_id":5,"from_port":0,"to_id":9}
1993:   ])";
1994: }
1995: 
1996: // And again, except that hub 5 says who it is speaking as, whatever the actor
1997: // walking onto it would have said.
1998: const char* AssignedJson() {
1999:   return R"([
2000:     {"type":"hub","conversation_id":0,"text":"start","tags":"start","flags":0},
2001:     {"type":"hub","conversation_id":5,"text":"assigned","expressions":"%speaker = \"Narrator\"","flags":0},
2002:     {"type":"hub","conversation_id":9,"text":"the end","flags":0},
2003:     {"type":"connection","from_id":0,"from_port":0,"to_id":5},
2004:     {"type":"connection","from_id":5,"from_port":0,"to_id":9}
2005:   ])";
2006: }
2007: 
2008: void TestAnAnonymousHubSpeaksAsWhoeverWalksOntoIt() {
2009:   std::printf("an anonymous hub speaks as whoever walks onto it\n");
2010: 
2011:   LouderName conversation = Declare("anonymous conversation");
2012:   std::string json = AnonymousJson();
2013:   CHECK(load_conversation_json(g_ctx, conversation, reinterpret_cast<uint8_t*>(&json[0]),
2014:                                json.size()) == 0,
2015:         "loading the anonymous conversation returned %d", 0);
2016: 
2017:   LouderName speak_a[1] = {Declare("A")};
2018:   LouderName speak_b[1] = {Declare("B")};
2019:   LouderActor actor_a = add_actor(g_ctx, conversation, 0, ActorFlags(), speak_a, 1, nullptr, 0);
2020:   LouderActor actor_b = add_actor(g_ctx, conversation, 0, ActorFlags(), speak_b, 1, nullptr, 0);
2021: 
2022:   // Put the two actors in their own channels, so that neither is dragged along
2023:   // by the other and each of them walks onto the hub on its own.
2024:   force_position(g_ctx, conversation, actor_a, 0, Declare("left"), 0);
2025:   force_position(g_ctx, conversation, actor_b, 0, Declare("right"), 0);
2026:   flush_events(g_ctx);
2027: 
2028:   // The hub carries no speaker of its own, which is what makes it anonymous.
2029:   CHECK(get_hub_speaker(g_ctx, conversation, 5) == LOUDER_NAME_NONE,
2030:         "hub 5 should carry no speaker of its own");
2031:

1: use super::context::LouderContext;
